V668: **FADING an ADDITIVE thing hides it; FADING an OPAQUE thing PAINTS BLACK.** T711: a beam is opaque scene geometry ∴ the lead's "fade the misses out at max range" would have drawn a **BLACK RIBBON over lit terrain**, ⊥ nothing. ∴ before dimming ANYTHING to hide it, ask which BLEND it draws through — for an opaque primitive the only honest hide is **⊥ DRAWING IT**. corollary measured same pass: recolouring a MISS class blinks worse than the hit class it replaces (0.4% → 11.3% churn) ∵ the CLASS BOUNDARY itself is what oscillates ∴ ⊥ recolour a binary verdict, DROP it|T711,T681,V638,V644
V669: **a gate on a BOUNDARY ! assert the BOUNDARY'S SHAPE — a downstream PIXEL check can be MASKED by ordering, defaulting ∨ OVERWRITE.** §V666 caught its own author: the §V665 namespace test **did ⊥ redden** when the prefix guard was reverted entirely, ∵ `registerMediaSource` **REPLACES on re-register** & the inference feed runs SECOND ∴ a media card wrongly claiming an `infer:` texture is silently OVERWRITTEN a moment later. the bug was real, the guard was right, & the pixel test **passed for a reason unrelated to what it claimed to assert**. ∴ replaced w/ a direct gate on WHICH SOURCE IDS EACH FEED CLAIMS (fake backend recording them), reddening in BOTH directions. general: test the CONTRACT AT THE SEAM, ⊥ its consequence 3 layers downstream|V666,V665,V655,T715
V670: **RELEASING a windowed file means COMMITTING your change — ⊥ merely CEASING TO EDIT.** T710 handed back `documents.ts` w/ its 465-line E13 hunk & its `look-baselines.json` row still UNCOMMITTED while it moved on to the md & tests ∴ the next holder (T711) could ⊥ `git commit -- <paths>` w/o **landing another worker's in-flight rebuild on their behalf** — §B133's exact hazard, & the §V494 pathspec rule forbids the index-staging workaround. ∴ a window CLOSES w/ a commit; if follow-up files remain, commit the WINDOWED file & continue outside it. corollary: a worker holding uncommitted edits ∈ a CONTESTED file blocks EVERY successor, ⊥ just the file|V494,B133,V643,T710,T711
V671: **a NEGATIVE result requires a PASSING CONTROL — a broken instrument reporting "absent" is INDISTINGUISHABLE from a real absence.** T715's 1st WebNN probe returned `navigator.gpu: undefined` from Playwright's Chromium ∈ ALL 3 configs INCLUDING headed w/ `--enable-unsafe-webgpu` — & **we KNOW WebGPU works ∵ the app runs on it** ∴ the CONTROL FAILED ∴ the negative was ⊥ evidence & the agent nearly reported it. re-run ∈ real Chrome 151 w/ the control PASSING (`navigator.gpu` object, adapter vendor `apple`, architecture `metal-3`): **`navigator.ml` = `undefined`** ∴ **WebNN is ⊥ PRESENT on the owner's machine — ⊥ flag-off-but-detectable, the ENTRY POINT does ⊥ EXIST.** ∴ ∀ "X is unavailable" claim ! carry a sibling "Y IS available" measured the SAME WAY|V461,V650,T715,T381
V672: **a capability label ! be LICENSED BY USE, ⊥ by SELECTION — & PAIRING the name w/ a MEASURED NUMBER makes it unable to lie about SPEED even where it cannot see the DEVICE.** @ detection time a path is CHOSEN but ⊥ TAKEN ∴ a bare "WebGPU" claims a path ⊥ yet exercised & "WebGPU · — ms" is a label w/ its evidence MISSING. ∴ the backend label appears ONLY after a completed inference; before that the node reports its STATE ("Acquiring model — 142 MB of 380 MB" | "Waiting for first result" | "Model unavailable — showing a flat depth map"). **self-correcting property**: ask transformers.js for `webnn-npu`, let it silently fall back INSIDE WebNN, & the label "WebNN" stays TRUE (we DID take that path) while **the LATENCY exposes the fallback**|V469,T715,T381
V673: **`memo` does ⊥ protect against CONTEXT — a context value re-renders EVERY consumer straight THROUGH `memo`.** T714: `NodeView` was memoized, its payload deliberately `{nodeId}`, & its projection reference-stable — all correct, all useless, ∵ it ALSO called `useGraphCanvas()`. ∴ **a memoized component's real dependency set is its props PLUS every context it reads**, & a context value rebuilt per revision defeats the whole memo strategy silently — the code looks right & the property is untrue (§V480's shape, one level up). ∴ ∀ context value shared by many consumers ! be reference-stable per member: read mutable state through a REF, & keep a derived collection's object when its MEMBERSHIP does ⊥ move|V480,T519,T714
V674: **a COUNTING gate ! subscribe to EXACTLY what the real component subscribes to.** T714's wrapper is `memo`'d **AND** calls `useGraphCanvas()` ∴ it re-renders under the 2 conditions `NodeView` does — **a wrapper that skipped the context would go QUIET during the very failure it exists to catch**. & it counts the thing that SCALES: renders on a 6-node vs a 60-node graph, asserting the DIFFERENCE does ⊥ grow (red-first **305 vs 35** = N+1 per edit, visible ∈ the numbers; after **0 and 0**), ⊥ a wall-clock threshold generous enough to survive noise & therefore generous enough to survive the regression returning|V666,V669,V649,T714
V675: **a feedback loop closing on the FINISHED FRAME smears EVERYTHING the frame contains ∴ a SELECTIVE trail closes on a SIDE BRANCH composited back.** E34 is the catalogue's 1st SELECTIVE loop (a luminance THRESHOLD decides what ENTERS it) & the 1st that does ⊥ close the way §V471.5 describes: it closes on `smear1`, a side branch added into the bloom's composite, ∵ closing on the output would trail the terrain, the chrome & the beams alike. ∴ **§V471.5 describes the DEFAULT, ⊥ the only shape** — & the roster ∈ `temporal.test.ts` is an EXPLICIT list, so adding a loop REDDENS it BY DESIGN (the gate working, ⊥ a sweep)|V471,T711,V631
V676: **an EXEMPTION silences a gate ∴ use one only when the GATE is wrong, NEVER when the PROSE is.** T716's md named `materialUnlit` ∈ a table describing a MUTATION, & E33's graph genuinely lacks the type ∴ a `DELIBERATE` entry would be **a gate declining to check a TRUE claim** — the prose gets rephrased instead. corollary from the same pass: when a rebuilt example inherits a measured table from the OLD form, **KEEP it w/ its ORIGINAL commit stamp & say WHAT it was measured on** — re-deriving produces a 3rd set of numbers for ONE invariant & makes the record worse, ⊥ better (§V641's stamp is what makes that legible)|V641,V643,T716,V640
V677: **⊥ MUTATE A SHARED SOURCE FILE TO TEST — mutate the GENERATED ARTIFACT the gate actually READS, & RESTORE BY REGENERATING.** T711's 1st mutation attempt was a `perl -i` on `documents.ts` **while 2 other workers had live hunks in it**, & it was a NO-OP BY LUCK: a shell expansion ate `${LIDAR_RANGE}` from BOTH pattern & replacement ∴ it substituted a string matching nothing. had it matched, it would have written into a contended file. **∴ the safe form is also the BETTER INSTRUMENT**: these gates read the `examples/` DIRECTORY via `listExamples()`, ⊥ the source ∴ mutating the SHIPPED `.loom.json` hits EXACTLY the surface the gates see & never touches a contended file. **restore is the GENERATOR, never a checkout** (§GOD-RULE). verify the tree clean after each pass|V666,V494,B133,T711 ⟶ **STRONGER FORM, used by T721: an ISOLATED `git worktree` — files copied in, `node_modules` symlinked, mutated THERE, worktree removed.** it needs ⊥ discipline about who else is typing & **the shared tree is never WRITTEN AT ALL** ∴ prefer it for SOURCE-level mutation; mutate-the-generated-artifact stays right for gates that read `examples/`
V678: **§V653 DEMONSTRATED, ⊥ argued: `liveness.test.ts` stayed GREEN under ALL TEN deliberate breakages** — including every ray drawn to full range, & 240 bounce legs (the green-spaghetti frame). @ 192×108 w/ a 10% band a look baseline **does ⊥ see structural damage this large** ∴ **a green baseline row means "about the same picture", ⊥ "this example is intact" — it is ⊥ a STRUCTURE gate & ⊥ cite it as one.** the claims are what catch structure; the baseline catches DRIFT|V653,V643,V666,T711
V681: **⊥ STILL-FRAME instrument can see MOTION damage — & most of ours are still-frame.** T716's most informative mutation (`z = 1 − 2u` → an INDEPENDENT sphere direction) **renders a PIXEL-IDENTICAL still frame** & re-deals EVERY tile in motion; ONLY the claim that names the correspondence sees it — ⊥ the rendered legibility gate, ⊥ the baseline, nothing else. same class as §V678's ten breakages surviving `liveness`. ∴ a property about CORRESPONDENCE ACROSS FRAMES (a tile keeping its identity, a marker holding its place, a beam matching its mark) ! be asserted STRUCTURALLY ∨ across FRAME PAIRS — a single rendered frame is evidence about a MOMENT, ⊥ about a MOTION|V678,V653,V666,T716,T711
V682: **a MULTI-RED can PROVE SHARING — if a mutation to a SHARED resolver reddens only ONE consumer, the consumers are ⊥ actually sharing.** T721's N5 mutated `resolveScalarMap` & reddened `points.test.ts`'s renderPoints `sizePixels` claim, `scene-pipeline.test.ts`'s geometry-map refusal, AND the new gate — **3 tests across 2 nodes** ∴ the resolver PROVED it is shared. had it reddened only the new gate, the 2 nodes would ⊥ actually answer "what may drive a size" the same way & **§V109 would be a COMMENT rather than a FACT**. ∴ §V666's "reddens it alone" is the rule for an ISOLATED claim; for a SHARED mechanism the multi-red IS the evidence — & either way the author ! be able to NAME which it is|V666,V109,T721
V683: **a UNIT TEST PINS THE CONVENTION ITS AUTHOR CHOSE ∴ it CANNOT DETECT THAT THE CONVENTION IS WRONG — only DOMAIN TRUTH can, & here only LOOKING did.** T704: the projector's LENS-SHIFT SIGN was INVERTED — positive `shiftY` slid the image **DOWN**. **EVERY unit test PASSED**, ∵ they pinned the author's chosen sign; Dawn gates were blind ∵ they use shift 0. the FIRST RENDERED E36 FRAME put the beams BELOW the cornice they were aimed past. ∴ for any parameter w/ an OUTSIDE convention (a projectionist's up/right, a photographer's f-stop, a musician's bar) the gate ! assert against **the DOMAIN's direction, named**, ⊥ against the implementation's own choice — & §V383 stands: LOOK at the first frame|V383,V681,V666,T704
V684: **the `text` node CANNOT appear ∈ a BASELINED example until the harness has a FONT story.** §V403: `registerSyntheticMediaSources` deliberately does ⊥ fake `text` — "black is the honest output of a machine w/ ⊥ font stack" — ∴ a text-bearing document renders **BLACK ∈ every offline gate** & its §V643 baseline would enshrine that black. ∴ E38 uses an ANALYTIC emblem (ramp/circle/noise composite), ⊥ `text`. **& this EXPLAINS §T728's list**: `text` is unused by any example ⊥ from neglect but ∵ it is UNGATEABLE — a node's absence from the catalogue can be a CONSEQUENCE, ⊥ an oversight, & is worth checking before treating coverage as a to-do|V403,T728,V643
V685: **a BACKTICK ∈ a WGSL COMMENT TERMINATES the TypeScript template literal — & it has bitten TWICE TODAY, 2 different workers.** T676's `RELIEF_LIFT_KERNEL` & T724's E33 kernels: writing `` `like this` `` in prose silently turns a kernel into a SYNTAX ERROR, & the error is `ERR_INVALID_TYPESCRIPT_SYNTAX` **pointing at a line of PROSE** ∴ it reads as unrelated. **EVERY WGSL constant ∈ `documents.ts` is exposed & NOTHING catches it before the regenerator runs** — & the tree is UNIMPORTABLE meanwhile, which the owner sees ∵ their dev server runs on this checkout. ∴ GATE IT (T729)|T676,T724,V670
V686: **a RECOVER-FROM-HISTORY splice needs an ANCHORED search & an assertion on the SPAN LENGTH.** T724's 1st apply script used a bare `pre.index('node(\n  "body",')` which matched a DIFFERENT example thousands of lines earlier & spliced a huge span into E33. it failed LOUDLY on a duplicate `const` ∴ caught ∈ seconds — but the ⊥-loud version of that mistake lands silently ∈ a 900-line file ⊥ one reviews line by line. ∴ anchor on something UNIQUE & assert the extracted span's LENGTH before writing|V494,V677,T724
V687: **an example whose SUBJECT IS CHANGE has ⊥ NULL STATE THAT LOOKS LIKE ANYTHING — if its performer does ⊥ move, it opens BLACK & reads as BROKEN.** a frame-difference probe rendered PURE BLACK ∵ `noise` has ⊥ intrinsic time axis (T518/§V624) & a static `t.z` differenced against a 6-frame-old copy is IDENTICALLY ZERO. ∴ for a motion example the UNDERSTUDY'S OWN MOTION is LOAD-BEARING, ⊥ decoration — & it is §V681's shape exactly: the claim is about CORRESPONDENCE ACROSS FRAMES ∴ assert across a frame PAIR, never from a still|V681,T518,V624,V411
V688: **polar/log-polar warp is EXPRESSIBLE TODAY ∈ 6 nodes — the lead's "most versatile missing primitive" hypothesis is REFUTED, by render.** `ramp(circular)` → `transform(s.x = 1/aspect, aspectcorrect off)` = θ aspect-corrected (`ramp(circular)` computes `atan2` ∈ **uv** space ∴ rays are ELLIPTICALLY spaced on 16:9 & the fix is exactly `atan2(dv, du·aspect)`); `circle(distance)` → `level(black=−k, white=0)` = ρ UNCLAMPED; `reorder` → `remap(extend:"repeat")`. ⚠**do ⊥ use `ramp(radial)` for ρ** — it is `clamp(length(uv−0.5)·2,0,1)` ∴ all 4 CORNERS (~20% of a 16:9 frame) pin to ONE FLAT VALUE, rendered & visibly dead. & RGB split (2 `transform` + 2 `reorder`), frame-difference (`cache`+`difference`+`level`), halftone, mosaic, luma key are ALL expressible. **feedback-with-transform ships TWICE already** (E1 fade+transform; E29 scale ABOVE ONE) ∴ a polar example ! ⊥ be a tunnel ∨ it collides w/ E29|T728,V624
V689: **a THRESHOLD chosen NEAR THE THING YOU ARE MEASURING turns ONE property into TWO — & can report the direction BACKWARDS.** T713's 1st sign test counted echo pixels above a BRIGHT threshold & said mid-focus had MORE area than either extreme — **exactly what an INVERTED SIGN looks like** — but a defocused mark is BIGGER **AND** DIMMER ∴ a bright gate measures the two TOGETHER & the area claim was contaminated by the brightness one. w/ a gate just clear of the BACKGROUND (area is the claim ∴ it ! ⊥ sit near the mark's own PEAK) the domain assertion PASSES: focus-near 17,102 px & focus-far 17,102 px, both above 14,591 for focus INSIDE the population ∴ **BOTH SIDES GROW, which is the property**. sibling of §V649 (percentage w/o population): **place the gate away from the QUANTITY YOU ARE ⊥ CLAIMING**|V649,V683,V666,T713
V690: **ADDING AN EXAMPLE TOUCHES 6 FILES + 2 CONDITIONAL — measured from `66653f7`, ⊥ recalled.** `src/examples/documents.ts` · generated `examples/E<N>-<Name>.loom.json` · hand-written `examples/E<N>-<Name>.md` · 1 row ∈ `examples/README.md` · 1 row ∈ `src/examples/look-baselines.json` (§V643, DELIBERATE) · 1 entry ∈ the HARD-CODED list ∈ `src/examples/runner.test.ts`. CONDITIONAL: `src/examples/temporal.test.ts` IF the graph closes a Feedback loop (its roster is explicit ∴ it reddens BY DESIGN), & a dedicated `*.gpu.test.ts` for a pixel claim. **BOTH REGISTRIES ARE DISCOVERY-DRIVEN & DELIBERATELY NEED ⊥ EDIT** — `catalogue.ts` `readdirSync`, `example-catalogue.ts` `import.meta.glob`. regenerators (neither wrapped ∈ a package script): `node --import ./src/mcp/alias-hooks.ts src/examples/build-examples.ts --only <N>` & the same for `measure-look-baselines.ts`|V643,T698,T730,V670
V691: **a CACHE keyed on a value that is CONSTANT ACROSS THE POPULATION is STRUCTURALLY BLIND.** `compileNow`'s plan cache compares by `graph.revision` ALONE & **all 29 shipped examples ship `revision: 1`** ∴ the comparison can NEVER distinguish any pair of them. it is masked today by render-before-effect ordering & the bus swap — masking, ⊥ correctness. ∴ before trusting a cache key, MEASURE ITS DISTRIBUTION over the real population; a key w/ ⊥ variance is ⊥ a key|B140,V649,T726
V692: **a REF read INSIDE a `.then` AFTER an `await` is ⊥ ∈ lockstep w/ the value it mirrors, whatever the comment says.** `use-frame-loop.ts:554` reads `documentBoundaryRef` after `await backend.compile`; its comment claims it "changes in lockstep w/ `compiled`" & it does ⊥ — any re-render producing `documentBoundary:false` WHILE the boundary compile is ∈ FLIGHT (the preview-sink store publishes on the graph pane's rAF tick, well inside a real compile) LOSES `resetTemporalHistory` + `seek(0)` for that load. **INTERMITTENT BY CONSTRUCTION ∴ the best remaining candidate for a genuine PIXEL leak, & ⊥ current gate can see it**|B141,V480,T726
V693: **an AUTO-RANGING plot whose WINDOW IS SHORTER THAN THE SIGNAL'S PERIOD rescales CONSTANTLY — & it is a CLIFF, ⊥ a slope.** measured, synthetic sine, frequency swept: window/period 0.022–0.30 → **~200 refits/min**; 0.50 → 142; **1.00 & above → ZERO**. ∴ the moment ONE PERIOD FITS ∈ the window the refits stop entirely. §T352's docblock states its own assumption in prose — *"a sliding window over a periodic signal varies by a fraction of a percent, so half is far outside the noise"* — **TRUE ONLY WHEN A PERIOD FITS ∈ THE WINDOW**, & nothing enforced that (§V478's shape). ∴ either the window CONTAINS a period, ∨ the range is HELD|V478,T352,T459,T735
V694: **a positive `blacklevel` IS A SUBTRACTION, `rgba16float` does ⊥ CLAMP, & `Level` is ROUTINELY REACHED FOR AS A THRESHOLD.** ⚠**WIDENED — the feedback loop was ⊥ the point, it only COMPOUNDS it every frame.** measured OUTSIDE a loop: `haze1` w/ `blacklevel 0.68` as a bloom threshold — "keep only the highlights" — sends every pixel below 0.68, which is NEARLY ALL OF THEM, negative as far as **−2.1** ∴ **the bloom SUBTRACTED everywhere it was ⊥ blooming**; fixing it took the frame's range **0.1147 → 0.9703**. E1's docstring ("crushes the dimmest survivors to zero") is TRUE ∈ unorm & FALSE ∈ the format we ship (§V478). ∴ **the NON-NEGATIVE way to threshold a bloom is `gamma1` BELOW 1** — `signedPow(c, 1/gamma)` crushes midtones & keeps highlights **w/o ever crossing zero**. ⚠ **the catalogue is ACCIDENTALLY SAFE & nobody wrote down why**: every shipped bloom uses blacklevel 0 ∨ 0.01, & E34's `poolbase` uses **−0.1 DELIBERATELY to LIFT** (§V644)|V478,V644,V698,V699
V695: **RAMP STOPS ARE DISPLAY SPACE & DECODE TO LINEAR ∴ a palette that looks BALANCED as authored numbers plays back WARM-OVER-BLACK.** measured: a dark cool stop `[0.05,0.36,0.55]` lands @ linear `[0.004,0.106,0.267]` & reads as BLACK — the decode costs a dark cool colour most of its LUMINANCE while barely touching a bright warm one. ∴ balance a palette by its LINEAR luminance, ⊥ by how its stops look ∈ the picker; the cool half ! be LIFTED until it carries comparable LIGHT. sibling of §V618 (the same encode, one layer up)|V618,V471,T-VJ
V696: **RANGE-CHECKING a gain/bias pair against its target means MEASURING the target, ⊥ ESTIMATING it — & the error is an ORDER OF MAGNITUDE, twice.** a `whitelevel 0.3` & later `0.045` were written against a frame-difference field whose measured max is **0.058**; the second mapped the field's MEDIAN to 0.86 & blew the frame WHITE. ∴ instrument the source's PERCENTILES before fitting anything to it — §V471's amended rule is only as good as the number you fit against|V471,V545,T-VJ
V697: **a PORTALED surface falls back to the GLOBAL keymap context ∴ DESTRUCTIVE GLOBAL bindings fire from INSIDE it — & a `<button>` is the ONE target shape the text-context rule CANNOT SEE.** T732 documented its strip's `stopPropagation` as stopping `tab` re-opening the browser on itself; **the mutation written to PROVE that DECLINED TO FAIL.** measured instead: the popover is PORTALED ∴ the keymap falls back to `environment.context`, which `app.tsx` sets to **`"global"`, ⊥ `"graph"`** — graph bindings cannot fire from in there AT ALL. **but GLOBAL ones can, & `mod+z` is `graph.undo`** ∴ the real hazard is **browsing categories w/ the keyboard SILENTLY UNDOING the user's last edit** — §V53's classic bug arriving through the one target the text-context rule misses. ∴ the gate is a NODE COUNT, ∵ the damage is to the DOCUMENT|V53,V78,V666,T732
V698: **an OPERATION'S ALGEBRAIC PROPERTY is a DETECTOR — use it before hunting.** T-VJ chased a contrast-floor failure through 3 wrong guesses (probe averaging, blur size, source distribution), each making it WORSE, then instrumented the per-stage histogram: `paint1` range **0.5454** → `burn1` range **0.1147**. **`burn1` is an ADD, & an ADD CANNOT REDUCE RANGE** ∴ one input was NEGATIVE — & that single fact named the fault immediately. ∴ when a pipeline's output violates a property its OPERATOR GUARANTEES, the violation LOCATES the fault; instrument per STAGE & compare against what each stage is ALLOWED to do|V666,V694,V649
V699: **`blur.size` is ∈ PIXELS while the liveness/contrast floor is measured @ 192×108 ∴ a halo that is a 2.7% glow @ 1280 is an 18%-OF-WIDTH WASH @ probe size.** §V627's resolution trap arriving through the BLOOM rather than through point density. ∴ ∀ pixel-denominated parameter ! be read against the resolution the GATE measures at, ⊥ only the one the owner SEES|V627,V643,V694
V700: **a UNIT gate proves a builder EXISTS; a BROWSER gate proves it carries the RIGHT INPUT; NEITHER ALONE IS SUFFICIENT.** T720's 3rd mutation is the finding — a builder of the **WRONG SHAPE** (`clickedNodeIds` sending `nodeIds` where the command reads `nodeId`) leaves the seam gate **GREEN** & the e2e **RED**, while removing the builder entirely reddens BOTH. ∴ presence & correctness are DIFFERENT CLAIMS needing DIFFERENT INSTRUMENTS — §V656's shape (right count, wrong order) one level up. & the near-miss worth keeping: the 1st e2e draft called `fitAll` before the right-click, `fitAll` → `focusGraph` CLICKS THE PANE & CLEARS THE SELECTION ∴ the test degenerated into the other case & **would have passed for the wrong reason** — caught by running it RED & reading WHY it was red, ⊥ by it going green|V656,V666,V634,T720
V701: **a gate that resolves its async on a MICROTASK never opens the window a REAL `await` opens ∴ it cannot see a race that lives ∈ that window.** §T519's `document-boundary.test.tsx` stayed **GREEN under all 3 of B141's mutations** ∵ it resolves `compile` on a microtask — **that is WHY B141 outlived the fix that was supposed to cover it**. ∴ a gate for a race ! OWN the window: defer the async explicitly & **assert 2 operations were genuinely OUTSTANDING TOGETHER** before claiming anything. & the jsdom limit was ⊥ fatal: the production TRIGGER needs rAF, the PROPERTY does ⊥ — drive the second compile by another real path & say so|V666,V681,T519,T733
V702: **LATCH OWED WORK @ SCHEDULE TIME, ⊥ @ RESOLVE TIME — a REF IS ⊥ A SNAPSHOT.** B141 read `documentBoundaryRef` inside a `.then` & got whatever the NEWEST render said; the mutation proving the placement matters: latching @ RESOLVE time instead of SCHEDULE time **still reddens the claim** ∴ the latch alone is ⊥ the fix, its PLACEMENT is. general: when an async result must act on the state that CAUSED it, capture that state where the work is SCHEDULED & treat it as a DEBT to discharge, ⊥ a flag to re-read|V692,V480,B141,T733
V703: **an invariant a READER ! REMEMBER is weaker than one a GATE CAN SEE — turn it into a gate wherever the shape allows.** E40 asserts STRUCTURALLY that **⊥ node on its feedback path carries a positive `blacklevel`** ∴ §V694 stops being prose someone has to recall & becomes a red test. cf §T675 (a new payload kind is a TYPECHECK failure until it declares an orbit) & §T721's `satisfies` table. ∴ when filing a §V, ask what SHAPE would make it enforceable, & say so ∈ the row|V694,V478,T675,T721
V704: **a property that is a CONJUNCTION cannot be SNIFFED FROM A SIGNATURE — DECLARE it.** `plotPeriodFollowsInputs` is "clockless **AND** stateless **AND** repeats-with-input", & ⊥ predicate over a `valueEvaluate` signature reveals that: `valueStep` reads `frame`, `channelIn` reads an AMBIENT BAG, `valueSwitch` may select a DIFFERENT INPUT moment to moment — **all 3 take a value input & NONE propagates a period**. ∴ declare it per node, & make ABSENT the safe default. general: when a capability is the conjunction of things that ⊥ appear ∈ the type, inference will be CONFIDENTLY WRONG on the counterexamples|V478,V109,T735
V705: **assert EXHAUSTIVENESS over the WHOLE UNION, ⊥ over the members CURRENTLY IN USE — a registry walk is BLIND to declared-but-unused kinds.** T712's table is `satisfies Record<PortKind, NodeFamily>` & its test asserts over the FULL union ∴ the 7 declared-but-unused kinds are covered & **the first `buffer`-output node would have arrived TINTED**; a walk over the 8 kinds in use would have said NOTHING about them. verified by DELETING a member & watching `tsc` NAME it. cf §T675 (a new payload kind is a typecheck failure until it declares an orbit) & §T721|V487,T675,T721,T712
V706: **when a published SHAPE does ⊥ fit your abstraction, GENERALISE YOUR OWN — ⊥ reshape shapes you do ⊥ own.** T719 wave 1: `FullscreenHolder` carries **2 slots** (T551 added `app` beside `current`) & `state-queries` holds `{ sources }`, never null, backing several QUERIES ⊥ one command. rather than reshape 2 published shapes w/ foreign call sites, the primitive became **`sharedForBus(bus, key, create)` — keyed on IDENTITY, ⊥ field layout** — w/ `commandHolder` a thin wrapper & **⊥ consumer's shape changed**. ⚠**the TELL that it is the right seam: wave 2's STORES want EXACTLY the same primitive** ∴ wave 2 is a CONTINUATION ⊥ a 2nd mechanism|V487,V109,T719
V707: **a mutation that catches NOTHING reveals a HOLE, ⊥ a safe area.** T719's completeness check ran ONE direction (a module still on the old holder). asking *"what would M4 catch?"* & finding it caught **nothing** exposed the other direction — **a module CONVERTED but MISSING FROM THE TABLE looks perfectly fine to the 1st check**. ∴ a completeness claim needs BOTH directions, & the way to find the missing one is to ask what each mutation would catch & disbelieve any that catches nothing. & 2 more from the same gate: **assert the PRECONDITION that the 2 module instances really are DISTINCT** (∨ a change ∈ module caching silently makes it one instance compared against ITSELF — green forever & blind), & **compare the OBJECTS, ⊥ the key STRINGS** (strings only prove someone TYPED 17 different things)|V666,V656,V700,T719 ⟶ **3rd instance (T727): its 1st E38 mutation was a NO-OP** — ∈ a §T401 PROCESSOR kernel `p.position` **IS** the upstream's position ∴ `fieldAt(p.position)` cannot express "sample where the mote is" & the mutation COULD ⊥ REDDEN. **that ⊥-reddening is the ONLY reason it discovered the gate it had just written was WORTHLESS**; the real mutation needed `p.position + p.drift` ⟶ **4th & 5th instances, T719 wave 2, BOTH on its own gate**: the no-op-unsubscribe mutation initially PASSED ∵ the probe called `begin(PROBE_NODE)` TWICE & the 2nd is an IDEMPOTENT no-op ∴ the assertion held whether ∨ ⊥ unsubscribe worked — `mutate` now ! produce genuinely DIFFERENT state; & a bad probe patched `{exposure: 2}` where the field is `exposureStops` ∴ the "mutation" mutated NOTHING & the store CORRECTLY declined to notify. **the gate caught its own probe, ⊥ review**
V708: **a GUARD'S SCOPE decides WHICH INSTANCES IT CAN SEE — & a scope chosen from the MECHANISM YOU ARE REASONING ABOUT can exclude the instance that MATTERS.** E40's §V694 guard, 1st draft, scoped to the ACCUMULATOR'S FEED PATH: it **reddened on `gain1` — HARMLESS, contained by Lookup's clamp-by-indexing — & stayed GREEN on `under1`, the HARMFUL one**, which fed `lay1` directly & would have thinned the wake over ANY footage w/ real blacks. **it caught the benign instance & missed the real one, & looked correct doing it.** restated over the WHOLE GRAPH it catches all 3 positive cases & still PERMITS a negative black level (a LIFT, legitimate — §V644) — & **that permissive case is exercised AS A MUTATION rather than assumed**, ∵ a guard scoped to make the current file pass is ⊥ a guard|V694,V666,V461,V703
V709: **2 changes that ARRIVE TOGETHER ! EACH be measured against THE OTHER ALONE — beating the BASELINE JOINTLY establishes NEITHER.** T734: the predecessor's weather+advection candidate beat SHIPPED E2 comfortably & that was TRUE; **what was never run is WEATHER vs ADVECTION-ALONE, ∵ advection-alone was ⊥ on its table** — the 2 arrived together. run properly, flattening the front **RAISES tile CV @ 7 of 8 ages** (lap mean 0.353 vs 0.277, **+28%**) & raises dark fraction @ ALL 8; motion is a wash (0.213 vs 0.226) ∴ **the front buys 6% of motion & costs 22% of composition**; & the slow swing it was ADDED FOR is IDENTICAL either way (dark 20.8 pts vs 21.1) ∵ **the advection ALONE already produces a swing of that size**. ∴ a joint candidate hides which half is carrying it, & the §V666 mutation on the wrong half **reddens NEITHER instrument** — which is §V707's tell|V666,V707,V626,T734
V710: **a MULTIPLY-based "weather" walks the chemistry DOWN, & down is the DENSEST regime — it moves a region TOWARD the owner's complaint, ⊥ away.** §V554's CORRECTED band: 0.00–0.30 is **DENSE LABYRINTH**. ∴ a front that multiplies does ⊥ walk a region OUT of the lattice into interesting territory — it walks it into **the most screen-filling regime there is**. measured on BOTH documents independently: E24 advect-only in-disc CV 0.430/0.237/0.307 vs advect+weather 0.300/0.232/0.249, w/ spot count **462/907/470 → 1120/2472/1631 — 3× the spots, finer, flatter, more even**. ∴ if weather is ever wanted here it ! push the coordinate **UP** toward the sparse/dying end, ⊥ down — & §V554 says >0.90 is DEAD, so the usable window is NARROW|V554,V626,V623,T734
V711: **`target` is a WGSL RESERVED KEYWORD — Dawn refuses the WHOLE MODULE, by name.** ⊥ recorded anywhere before T727 hit it, & cheap to trip ∵ it is the obvious identifier for exactly the thing a point kernel computes|V685,T727
V712: **§V678 DEMONSTRATED AGAIN & HARDER: giving EVERY ribbon THE WRONG OWNER moves the look instrument's range 0.4129 → 0.4125** — straight through the 10% band. & on E38: **6,528 members become 8,302, 3,573 slots CHANGE SIDES, & the look range reads 0.8953 for BOTH to FOUR DECIMALS.** ∴ **a look baseline cannot see a TOTAL RE-ASSIGNMENT of which element is which.** the CLAIMS caught both; the baseline caught NEITHER ∴ ⊥ cite a green look row as evidence about STRUCTURE ∨ CORRESPONDENCE|V678,V681,V643,T727
V713: **RUNNING e2e AGAINST THE SHARED DEV SERVER IS NON-DETERMINISTIC while other workers edit the tree** — 4 failures then 1 on CONSECUTIVE IDENTICAL runs. ∴ the standard for e2e ∈ this tree is **`git archive HEAD` into a temp dir + an ISOLATED server + your own files on top**, ⊥ only for attribution but for EVERY run. (& it is how 3 workers have now proven "⊥ mine" by measurement rather than adjacency)|V620,V646,T719
V714: ⚠**`git commit --amend` ALWAYS REWRITES **HEAD**, whatever pathspec you give it — & ∈ a shared tree HEAD MAY ⊥ BE YOURS.** ⚠**LEAD ERRATUM on this row's 1st draft**: I wrote that amend "reads the shared index" & produced a SECOND commit. **FALSE.** verified: `b018aa7^ = 1c8cbd4^ = 9ec501a`, `tree(1c8cbd4) = tree(b018aa7)`, `git diff` EMPTY ∴ **`b018aa7` IS `1c8cbd4` w/ its MESSAGE REPLACED** — ⊥ a 2nd commit, & `1c8cbd4` is **ORPHANED**, reachable only via reflog. **a pathspec selects CONTENT, NEVER WHICH COMMIT ∴ it cannot protect you.** ⚠**& the DAMAGE is ⊥ attribution — it is the REASONING RECORD**: ∈ this project the commit MESSAGE is where the reasoning lives, & the rewrite erased a 4-mutation §V666 table (incl the *same object, lost subscribers* row that is the ENTIRE argument for the 2nd gate), a self-correction, & 2 §V707 self-catches. **& it is the WORST of the 9 sweep routes ∵ it FAILED SILENTLY**: the amend SUCCEEDED & produced a well-formed commit carrying the amender's message — the other 8 left evidence AT THE POINT OF THE MISTAKE. ∴ **⊥ `--amend` ∈ this tree, ever** — & preserve an orphan w/ a LIGHTWEIGHT TAG (`gc.reflogExpireUnreachable` = **30 days**), which is additive & reversible|V494,B145,V670
V715: **work that ! happen AFTER an await is LATCHED AS A DEBT (§V702); work that ! happen BEFORE it is MOVED EARLIER — ⊥ latched.** §B143's fix is §V702's COMPLEMENT & the distinction is the point: B141's boundary work ran after `await backend.compile` ∴ a ref could move under it ∴ it had to be OWED. B143's had to be gone BEFORE the compile installs ∴ the answer is a DOOR THAT OPENS EARLY ENOUGH — a commit-time effect — & **⊥ supersession risk exists ∵ nothing is deferred: a React effect cannot be outrun by a promise scheduled ∈ the SAME COMMIT**. ⊥ new mechanism was added beside §V702's; the existing block MOVED|V702,V701,B143
V716: **a TOLERANCE on a QUANTISED measurement ! be expressed ∈ QUANTA — a precision-digit tolerance landing BETWEEN lattice points is MARGINAL BY CONSTRUCTION.** §B144: a value on a `k/255` lattice (1 count = 0.00392) against a prediction of **36.43 counts**, gated by `toBeCloseTo(…,2)` = ±0.005 = **1.28 counts** ∴ it admits exactly 2 lattice points & rejects their neighbours by 0.0006 → **~50% flake**. ∴ (a) compute the QUANTUM & size the window in it; (b) **widen by WINDOW, ⊥ by PRECISION DIGIT** — a digit is a 10× jump & the next one down (±0.05) would have stopped refuting the hypothesis the gate exists to test; (c) a flake that is marginal-by-construction reads as "statistical" & is ⊥ — **the tell is the failures landing on ADJACENT LATTICE POINTS rather than scattering**|V649,V666,B144
V717: **a BASELINE SAMPLED AT EARLY FRAMES CANNOT SEE A LATE-ONSET FAILURE — & §V643's rows are sampled @ f60–f180.** T734 measured it: **the look baseline's motion row moved 5% while the picture's LATE-AGE motion moved 10×**, ∵ the instrument looks BEFORE the collapse. & the collapse IS the owner's complaint — E2's composition dies @ **f600** & holds for the next fifty seconds. ∴ **a baseline row is evidence about the OPENING of a document, ⊥ about its BEHAVIOUR**; a fault that arrives after the sampling window is invisible to it BY CONSTRUCTION. 3rd distinct blindness recorded for the same instrument: §V678 (structural damage), §V712 (correspondence), & now ONSET|V678,V712,V643,T734
V718: **a MEAN OF CONSECUTIVE DIFFERENCES TELESCOPES TO THE ENDPOINTS — it CANNOT see whether the step is CONSTANT.** §B144's assertion: every gain step is 10 dB ∴ `slopes[i] = means[i−1] − means[i]` & their mean is EXACTLY `(first − last)/4` ∴ it depends **only on the 2 endpoints**, while the comment claimed it pinned *"a FIXED step per 10 dB"*. the per-step values genuinely VARY (0.1127–0.1654 on `low`, ~10 FFT bins @ 23.4 Hz) & that variation is PRINTED & never ASSERTED. ∴ **CONSTANCY needs its OWN assertion & cannot be smuggled into an average** — & a comment describing what a statistic MEANS ! be checked against what it COMPUTES|V666,V649,B144
V719: **a tolerance set INSIDE the statistic's own OBSERVED RANGE is the original error one size down.** §B144's 1st patch shipped ±0.01 & passed 32/32 — then a control run produced **0.13034, which is 0.0125 from the prediction & OUTSIDE that window**. widened to ±0.015 to cover the observed spread w/ margin, & **falsified in BOTH directions** (a predicted span of 60 ∨ 80 dB still reddens 4/4) ∴ the widening did ⊥ buy green by giving up discrimination. **the author reported nearly repeating the mistake rather than landing the number that passed its own runs**|V716,V666,B144
V720: **`vec3f` & `vec4f` cost THE SAME — 16 bytes each — ∵ WGSL ALIGNS A VEC3 TO 16.** ∴ ∀ "a 3-component form is cheaper" argument about an attribute is FALSE HERE, & a choice framed as a cost trade is really a CAPABILITY choice w/ ⊥ price attached. the lead framed §T723 as "quaternion vs direction is what decides it" & the measurement removed the decision's premise entirely|V588,T723
V721: **a READ path ! have ⊥ ROUTE TO THE NETWORK — make the expensive path UNREACHABLE BY CONSTRUCTION, ⊥ by discipline.** §T383: `refresh` reads the cache & CANNOT open a connection; `acquire` is the ONLY thing that can ∴ **placing a node cannot spend 94 MB** no matter what calls it. & 2 siblings from the same design: **a TRUNCATED download is a FAILURE, ⊥ a smaller model** (caching it poisons the machine & surfaces far away as a parse error naming nothing); & the network cost is paid on an EXPLICIT press, never on a placement|V585,T383
V722: **a CANCEL is ⊥ a FAILURE — a red row for a choice the USER MADE is the noise that teaches people to IGNORE the surface.** §T383 returns to **ABSENT** on cancel, ⊥ to *failed*. general: reserve the loud state for things the user did ⊥ choose & cannot fix by repeating themselves|V469,V519,T383
V723: **a COUNT ∈ A TEST NAME GOES STALE SILENTLY** — `reproducibility.test.ts` was titled *"classifies exactly four nodes"* while holding FIVE since T654. same defect as §B137's header prose. ∴ NAME the members ∨ assert the count against the SOURCE, never write the number ∈ prose a test cannot check|B137,V478,V705
V724: **a flag that changes DETERMINISM ! default to the DETERMINISTIC side — pick the default by WHICH FAILURE IS LOUDER.** §T740 found the trap LIVE: `renderFrameRange` (`src/app/render-range.ts:142`) steps the **LIVE** clock — `seek(start)` then `stepOnce()` per frame w/ a GPU readback & a video encode BETWEEN steps; so do `seek`'s 0..N replay (§V170) & the step button. ∴ wall-measured stepping makes any step over ~25 ms worth TWO frames & **a slow machine renders a SHORTER, TIME-COMPRESSED FILE from the same project, SILENTLY**. ∴ `presenting?: () => boolean` **defaults FALSE**: forgetting to opt IN costs a slow clock (AUDIBLE, & gated); forgetting to opt OUT corrupts a render (SILENT, & shipped). `use-frame-loop.ts:375` binds it to `driverRef.current?.running === true` ∴ ∀ deterministic caller runs against a STOPPED driver|V662,V170,V437,T740
V725: **an ASSERTION CAN BE THE BUG AT REST — when a fix contradicts a GREEN test, the test is a SUSPECT, ⊥ an authority.** §T740 narrowed 3 claims rather than deleting them: *"timeline advances 1/fps REGARDLESS OF WHEN FRAMES ARRIVED"* → *"regardless of rAF's JITTER"*; & the wall-pair test's *"timeline advanced 1/60 while wall advanced 0.25s"* — **that assertion WAS the defect, written down & passing**. ∴ NARROW to what the claim MEANT; a green test that encodes the bug is worse than ⊥ test ∵ it defends it|V478,V705,T740
V726: **⊥ RANDOMISED INPUTS ∈ a test — they hide a flake as a pass rate.** §T740's old jitter test used `Math.random() * 40`, which under the corrected clock would have been **flaky @ ~39% pass** ∴ read as "a real bug, sometimes" by whoever next ran it. replaced w/ a FIXED ±5 ms arrival sequence. a test ! fail for one reason, deterministically|V713,V666,T740
V727: **∀ provider DECLARED UNREACHABLE ! NAME WHAT WOULD UNBLOCK IT — GATED, ⊥ prose.** §T736 ships a gate requiring it ∴ **a deferral cannot become PERMANENT BY FORGETTING**. this is §V205's lesson moved from FACTORIES to PLATFORMS: an absent thing that states its own removal condition stays a CHOICE the owner can see; one that silently FALLS BACK removes the choice by HIDING it. corollary, §T736's own finding: **⊥ SHIM A PLATFORM PATH THAT DOES ⊥ EXIST** — a provider that pretends is worse than a slot that says "this platform ships nothing reachable, here is what would change that"|V205,V721,T736,T385
V728: **§V585's claim of ⊥ new resource kind & ⊥ new GPU→CPU route was TRUE FOR DEPTH ∵ DEPTH'S OUTPUT IS A TEXTURE — it was NEVER TESTED against a NON-TEXTURE output.** §T743 found the wall & verified ∀ door ⊥ inferred: (1) `registerMediaSource` is the ONLY CPU→GPU path ∈ the whole backend contract, ⊥ buffer equivalent; (2) `external` scratch is TEXTURE-ONLY (kinds = `bufferPair`,`buffer`,`external`,`ring`; `external` takes a `sourceId` + a `TextureFormat`); (3) **⊥ point node creates POSITIONS from a texture** — `textureToAttribute` writes a FIXED `sample` attribute ∴ cannot relocate points, & `pointKernelAdvanced` has ⊥ texture input at all. ∴ an invariant proven on ONE output shape carries a SCOPE CONDITION even when its text does ⊥ say so; state the condition when the 2nd case arrives, ⊥ retire the invariant|V585,T743,T386
V729: **a claim about a VALUE THAT NEVER REACHES THE SCREEN CANNOT BE MADE FROM PIXELS — READ THE BUFFER.** §T741: a §V683 claim about a ROTATION is unprovable from a frame ∵ lighting, silhouette & symmetry all launder it. ∴ `renderHeadless` gains an optional **`probeBuffers`** seam — named buffer readback after the final frame, ADDITIVE, 0 change for existing callers. this is the 10th reader-that-cannot-see & the 1st fixed by giving the gate a SECOND SENSE rather than a better eye. general: when a property is INVISIBLE BY CONSTRUCTION, do ⊥ strengthen the pixel assertion — ADD THE CHANNEL THAT CAN SEE IT|V683,V712,V620,T741,T723
V730: **ONE DECISION, ONE SAMPLING SITE.** §T744: the spawn HOOK refuses `fieldAt` BY NAME even though binding it would have been easy — the kernel samples the field ONCE to decide the birth & stashes what the child needs ∈ an attribute, & the copy pass carries it. 2 sampling sites for 1 decision means **the 2 can DISAGREE** (different frame, different filtering, different uv) & the disagreement is invisible ∵ both look correct alone. ∴ when a capability could be offered @ 2 points ∈ a pipeline, offer it @ the point that DECIDES & make the other REFUSE BY NAME w/ a message saying why|V349,V588,T744,T401
V731: **when 2 BUGS SHARE 1 SYMPTOM, the diagnostic's PAYLOAD IS THE VERDICT, ⊥ the fields — & VERDICTS ! CARRY PRECEDENCE.** §T739: `store-collapsed` **BEATS EVERY LATER VERDICT** ∵ a HEALTHY runtime blitting into a 1×1 store would otherwise read as `presenting-black` & **send the reader @ the SHADER when the fault is a DEAD OBSERVER**. fields alone let a reader pick the wrong story; an ordered verdict cannot. & the instrument ! be a PURE CONSUMER off the hot path (§V7) & read what a HUMAN SEES, ⊥ the linear buffer (§V618). corollary: a good instrument surfaces an outcome the FILER DID ⊥ LIST — §T739's `presenting` w/ `luma > 0` (fault DOWNSTREAM of all 3 suspects: CSS, stacking, window compositing) was ∈ ⊥ hypothesis & is the reading the instrument exists to catch|V7,V469,V618,V655,T739,T705
V732: **A BASELINE CAN RATIFY A DEFECT — a baseline delta HAS NO SIGN, & only the FRAME decides which way it points.** §T745: `f0max` **DROPPED 0.6352 → 0.0687**, which reads as a REGRESSION & is a FIX — the OLD frame 0 carried a TRANSIENT FLASH (the cache-empty first difference lit the whole frame & the recycle gate birthed a burst off it) & **that flash was BAKED INTO THE BASELINE & PASSING**. the new `firstRun` frame spawns nothing ∴ frame 0 is now HONESTLY the empty stage the md always claimed. ∴ ∀ baseline move → **LOOK AT THE FRAME & state the direction ∈ the commit**; a number alone cannot tell a correction from a regression, & a baseline that has ratified a bug DEFENDS it exactly as §V725's green test does|V725,V471,V717,T745
V733: **A TEST STAND-IN ! BE SIZED BY THE FORMAT IT FILLS, ⊥ by a hardcoded stride.** §T743: the harness stand-in hardcoded **4 BYTES/TEXEL**; pose's keypoint map is `rgba16float` at **8**. Dawn REFUSED outright — `Required size for texture data layout (136) exceeds the linear data size (68)` — & **that was the LUCKY outcome**: a fake that happened to be the RIGHT LENGTH for the WRONG FORMAT would have uploaded GARBAGE SILENTLY & surfaced months later as a scrambled skeleton. ∴ a stand-in that encodes an assumption the real thing does ⊥ share is a BUG WITH A DELAY FUSE|V713,V469,T743
V734: **a node that reads AT an address ≠ a node that PRODUCES an address — ⊥ overload the 2 onto one type.** §T743: `textureToAttribute` samples AT a point's position ∴ **position is the ADDRESS**; `pointsFromTexture` lets the texture DECIDE position ∴ **position is the RESULT**. overloading would have the node **READ AT THE ADDRESS IT IS ABOUT TO OVERWRITE**. the surgical-change preference (fewest new types) LOSES here to a real semantic split — & the split is visible ∈ the DATA FLOW, ⊥ ∈ taste|V427,V585,V728,T743,T386
V735: **A CONSTANT LAG IS REPRODUCIBLE; A VARIABLE LAG IS ⊥. THE DEFECT IS THE VARIANCE, ⊥ THE DELAY.** §T747: before, a take sampled whatever the model happened to be behind by — **wall-clock dependent, different on every run & every machine, w/ nothing ∈ the file saying so**. after: **exactly 1 frame, everywhere, every time**. ∴ when latency cannot be removed, MAKE IT CONSTANT & STATE IT; a known fixed offset is a property a reader can correct for, an unknown varying one is CORRUPTION THAT LOOKS LIKE OUTPUT. & ⊥ buy zero-lag w/ a 2nd render pass: re-running a frame ADVANCES EVERY STATEFUL NODE AGAIN, which is a WORSE & INVISIBLE error|V170,V701,V724,T747
V736: **when a WARNING'S CLAUSE becomes true of only SOME of its subjects, SPLIT IT — ⊥ keep 1 sentence that is HALF FALSE.** §T747: the async clause covered `analyze`/`channelIn` AND `depth`/`pose`, & went false for the latter the moment the settle landed. a warning that keeps asserting a fixed limitation is **the stale warning people learn to IGNORE**, & it takes the TRUE half down with it. ∴ split by CAUSE, let the still-unfixed half say so ALONE & VERBATIM, & gate both directions incl the document that contains both|V469,V722,V620,T747
V737: **assert the operations were GENUINELY OUTSTANDING TOGETHER — ⊥ that the ORDER CAME OUT RIGHT afterwards.** §T747's M2 mutation is why: `void settle(...)` FIRE-AND-FORGET **looks like it settles**, & an ordering-only assertion PASSES it. ∴ a concurrency gate ! observe the window (this frame's settle ENTERED, nothing encoded for it), ⊥ the outcome. & hold that window open w/ a `setTimeout` deferred: **a gate resolving on a MICROTASK never opens the window a real `await` opens** ∴ it proves nothing about real async|V701,V666,V655,T747
V738: **A RED-VERIFY RESTORES BY EDIT, NEVER BY CHECKOUT — & the OWNER'S git ban is ABSOLUTE, ⊥ a heuristic to be weighed against convenience.** §B147: the mutation you introduced to prove a gate REDDENS is 30 seconds old & 1 token wide ∴ **re-apply the fix the same way you broke it**. the danger of `checkout` here is ⊥ the intent, it is the BLAST RADIUS THE COMMAND HAS REGARDLESS OF INTENT — ∈ a SHARED TREE w/ concurrent workers (§B134) the same keystroke that restores your token discards someone else's uncommitted file. ∴ ⊥ destructive git, ⊥ exceptions, & a worker who slips ! SAY SO — §B147 was self-reported & that is the ONLY reason it can become a rule instead of a habit|V713,B134,B147,B145
V739: **A DECORATIVE TEST PASSES FOR A REASON UNRELATED TO ITS CLAIM — the FIXTURE ! EXERCISE THE MECHANISM IT CLAIMS TO BYPASS.** §T729: an ignores-non-shaders fixture written w/ **SQL `--` comments** could ⊥ trigger the WGSL comment rule EVEN IF THE DETECTION WERE DELETED ∴ it asserted nothing & looked green forever. ∴ ∀ negative test ! be MUTATION-CHECKED against the very check it exercises — a test that survives DELETING its subject is DECORATION. distinct from §V725 (an assertion that encodes the bug): this one encodes NOTHING|V666,V655,V725,T729 ⟶ **AMENDED by §T764, & the 3RD self-catch of this class ∈ one session**: a red-verify that PASSES means EITHER the check is unneeded **∨ YOUR SCENARIO IS PROTECTED BY SOMETHING ELSE** — §T764's first fixture passed w/o the identity check ∵ a healthy-device swap is **SAFE BY ORDERING**, ⊥ ∵ the check was pointless. ∴ ⊥ conclude a check is unnecessary until you have found the window where it IS necessary & shown the fixture REACHES it. the other 2 this session: §T729's SQL-comment fixture & §T759's invented depth map
V740: **PATH-SCOPED COMMITS PROTECT YOU FROM SWEEPING OTHER FILES — ⊥ FROM YOUR OWN HUNK LEAVING ∈ SOMEONE ELSE'S COMMIT.** §T730's README edit rode out ∈ §T749's `6a5b0ab` ∵ that worker legitimately needed the SAME FILE. **the DETECTION IS ASYMMETRIC**: §V468's pre-commit check inspects YOUR diff for FOREIGN hunks & **cannot see YOUR hunk departing ∈ another's commit**. & the file was ⊥ on any contested list ∵ **nobody thinks of a README as contested**. ∴ ∈ a shared tree, COMMIT SMALL EDITS IMMEDIATELY rather than holding them across a task, & treat DOCS as contended as code. inverse direction of §B134|V468,B134,B147,T730,T749
V741: **⊥ BUILD A GATE ON AN INTERNAL ∨ UNPUBLISHED API — a gate that STOPS GATING is WORSE THAN ⊥ GATE.** §T729 chose the public `transpileModule` over `SourceFile.parseDiagnostics`, which WORKS TODAY, is absent from the published types, & would break **SILENTLY** on a dependency upgrade — leaving a green suite that checks NOTHING. same family as §V739: both are gates that LOOK like they are watching|V739,V478,T729
V742: **A HERMETIC FAKE NEVER DISAGREES W/ THE REAL THING ∵ IT NEVER MEETS IT — ∀ external artifact ! have ≥1 CONTRACT TEST AGAINST THE ARTIFACT ITSELF.** §B148: 32 green tests around a node that could ⊥ run, ∵ every one stopped at the model's edge & the replay feed agreed w/ whatever the packer produced. the fix is ⊥ more hermetic gates — it is ONE gate that reads the REAL signature (dtype, dims, channel count) & fails when the packer disagrees. cheap, ⊥ a full inference. same shape as §V733's stand-in sized by a hardcoded stride: **a fake that encodes an assumption the real thing does ⊥ share is a BUG W/ A DELAY FUSE**|V733,V713,V469,B148
V743: **READ A MODEL'S SIGNATURE FROM THE ARTIFACT, ⊥ FROM ITS CARD — DOCUMENTATION DESCRIBES THE ANCESTOR.** §B148: MoveNet's card documents the TENSORFLOW original (`int32`, 3 channels) while the ONNX WEB EXPORT takes `uint8` & 4. the card was ⊥ lying; **it was describing a DIFFERENT ARTIFACT w/ the same name**. ∴ for ∀ 3rd-party binary — model, codec, font, wasm — the FILE is the contract & the prose is a HINT, & the docblock ! record WHICH ONE was consulted|V478,V742,B148
V744: **MEASURE BEFORE BUILDING, & LET THE MEASUREMENT BE ALLOWED TO KILL THE TASK.** §T382 was ordered as measure-first ∵ the hitch had been NAMED twice & TIMED never. the number came back **2599 ms** & the branch was BUILD — but the SAME instruction on a 18 ms number would have DEFERRED the task, & the run FOUND §B148 ON THE WAY, which ⊥ amount of building would have. ∴ an unmeasured performance fix is SPECULATION w/ a commit message|V620,V713,T382,B148
V745: **A SMALLER ARTIFACT IS A SMALLER DOWNLOAD, ⊥ A FASTER ONE — ⊥ INFER SPEED FROM SIZE.** §T753: `q4f16` depth is **1.44× SLOWER** than fp32 & int8 pose **3.7× slower**, ∵ unpacking quantized weights costs more than the memory traffic it saves. ∴ a size ratio is EVIDENCE ABOUT BANDWIDTH ONLY; ∀ speed claim ∈ user-facing copy ! cite a MEASUREMENT ∨ ⊥ be made. §B149 shipped exactly this claim, unmeasured, ∈ 2 node descriptions|V743,V744,B149,T753
V746: **A CONTRACT FIXTURE ! BE EXTRACTED FROM THE ARTIFACT, ⊥ TYPED BY HAND — a hand-written fixture CARRIES THE SAME WRONG ASSUMPTION FROM THE SAME SOURCE.** §T382: `extract-model-signatures.ts` reads dtype/dims/channels from the REAL weights; a fixture typed from the model card would have reproduced §B148's error VERBATIM & the gate would have PASSED THE BUG. 2 halves ∵ neither alone suffices: an ALWAYS-ON test catches a PACKER DRIFTING from a recorded signature; an OPT-IN test against the real weights catches a SIGNATURE WRONG WHEN WRITTEN. verified BOTH ways — opt-in passes against the real 125 MB, & reverting §B148's fix reddens the always-on half|V742,V743,V566,B148
V747: **PUT THE DECISIONS ∈ A CORE MODULE & MAKE THE THREAD FILE A SHIM — a module reachable ONLY BY STARTING A THREAD CANNOT BE UNIT-TESTED.** §T382 splits `inference-worker-core` from the worker entry ∴ every decision is testable w/o a `Worker`. **wired-but-never-tested is §V220's bug wearing its other face**. NB the entry point is ⊥ walked by §V205's scan — same gap as `createHeadlessMcpServer` — ∴ `createWorkerCore` is DECLARED there|V220,V205,V437,T382
V748: **AN EXTRACTION ! REPRODUCE THE ORIGINAL BYTE-FOR-BYTE AT THE ORIGINAL'S PARAMETERS — that IDENTITY is what makes it a SHARE rather than a REWRITE.** §T379 moved the tile's gesture arithmetic into `orbit-gestures.ts` & **@ scale 1 it is the old constants byte-for-byte**, w/ the new width-normalised scale the ONLY added degree of freedom. ∴ when unifying N copies into 1, PIN the identity @ the incumbent's settings & assert it; w/o that pin a *shared* mechanism is a SILENT BEHAVIOUR CHANGE ∈ every consumer at once, & the regression appears ∈ the one surface nobody re-checked|V255,V566,V655,T379,T561
V749: **A GATE THAT IS RED FOR EVERYONE IS A GATE NOBODY RUNS — a persistent failure is EVIDENCE ABOUT THE PROCESS, ⊥ about the code.** §B150: 2 lint errors survived from E38 to today ∵ **every worker lints only their OWN TOUCHED FILES** ∴ the directory command is nobody's habit & its red is nobody's problem. ∴ when a failure is found to be OLD, the finding is **HOW IT SURVIVED**, ⊥ the failure — & the fix ! change WHO RUNS WHAT, ⊥ only the 2 lines. corollary: **a worker who spots a pre-existing red ∈ ANOTHER row's file ! FLAG IT, ⊥ silently fix it** (§V740's tree hygiene) — but the ORCHESTRATOR ! then ROUTE it, else the flag rots exactly as the red did|V620,V740,V478,B150
V750: **WHEN A CAPABILITY LANDS, AUDIT THE WORKAROUNDS IT OBSOLETES — a COMPENSATION for an ABSENT capability becomes an ARTIFACT once the capability EXISTS.** §T758: E13's beams sat IN FRONT of the prism body ∵ **an OPAQUE solid would have swallowed them** — correct while the body was opaque, & the CAUSE of a refracted ghost-double the moment the body became transmissive. moving them INSIDE was ⊥ a tweak, it was **DELETING A WORKAROUND WHOSE REASON HAD EXPIRED**. ∴ ∀ new capability ! be followed by a search for the code that EXISTED TO SURVIVE ITS ABSENCE; that code is now the bug, & it looks like working code|V421,V471,T758,T725
V751: **LOOSENING A CLAIM IS LEGITIMATE ONLY W/ THE MEASUREMENT SHOWING THE DISCRIMINATION SURVIVES.** §T758 loosened §T710's fan-burial from `0` to `<30` & attached BOTH numbers: the new true value is **3**, & the RED-VERIFIED real burial measures **209** ∴ **2 orders of separation retained**. w/o the 2nd number a loosened bound is indistinguishable from a claim being QUIETLY DISARMED to stay green — which is §V725's defect arriving by a slower route. ∴ state the NEW true value AND the value the claim ! still reject|V725,V666,V655,V732,T758,T710
V752: **A GATE THAT IS NOISY LOCALLY WILL ⊥ BE RUN, HOWEVER CLEAN CI IS — THE NOISE IS THE DEFECT.** §B150: `pnpm lint` showed **359 errors locally, 357 of them throwaway scratchpad probes** ∴ ∀ worker who DID run the project gate saw a wall of unrelated red & correctly inferred it carried ⊥ signal — so nobody watched the ONE place (CI) where the signal was clean. **the 2 real errors survived ~100s of commits ⊥ ∵ people are careless but ∵ THE GATE WAS UNUSABLE.** ∴ when a check goes unrun, fix its SIGNAL-TO-NOISE & its DISCOVERABILITY before blaming habit; & an IGNORE LIST ! MOVE W/ THE CONVENTION IT SERVES (§B153: `.probe/**` stayed ∈ the list after probes moved to `scratchpad/`)|V749,V478,B150,B153
V753: **A GATE ADDED TO CI IS ⊥ A GATE ANYONE RUNS — & PASSING ONE GATE SAYS NOTHING ABOUT ANOTHER.** §B151: `pnpm test` (5912 green) & `pnpm typecheck` (0 errors) were BOTH clean while **`pnpm build` had been RED FOR 7 COMMITS & the app did ⊥ BUILD**; §B152's inverse: `pnpm test` GREEN everywhere anyone runs it & RED ∈ the fresh checkout nobody makes. ∴ **§V620 gate honesty ! NAME WHICH GATES RAN**, & a worker touching BUILD-TIME surface (a `new Worker`, an import shape, an asset URL) ! run `pnpm build` — the test runner transforms differently from the bundler & ⊥ suite size substitutes|V620,V713,B151,B152
V754: **A NEW REASON TO DO X ! BECOME A NEW INPUT TO THE EXISTING POLICY, ⊥ A NEW POLICY.** §T756: the viewer needed a hidden node kept alive, & the scheduler ALREADY had `pinned` — which bypasses visibility & ranks first. ∴ the fix was **ONE MORE REASON TO PIN**, ⊥ a viewer-specific keep-alive ∴ 1 assembler, 1 policy, & the popout inherited it FREE. the test of the shape: **a 3rd consumer arrives w/ 0 special-casing**. a parallel policy would have had to re-derive visibility, ranking & budget, & would drift from the original the first time either changed|V730,V748,V461,T756,T744
V755: **VERIFY A BUILD FIX BY RUNNING THE ARTIFACT, ⊥ BY THE BUILDER'S EXIT CODE.** §T760: rollup accepting `worker.format:"es"` says only that ROLLUP IS SATISFIED — a config that pleases the bundler while emitting a worker the browser refuses to START would look FIXED & be the SAME CLASS OF DEFECT that caused §B151. ∴ the fix was proven by SERVING `dist/` & loading it ∈ a real browser until the worker POSTED A MESSAGE BACK. general: for ∀ defect whose signature was *the tests passed & the thing did ⊥ work*, the verification ! reach the LAYER THAT FAILED. corollary §B151/§B152: **∀ CI gate ! be provable ∈ a `git archive HEAD` tree** — green on a dev machine is green ∈ an environment that has accumulated state nobody declared|V753,V713,V620,B151,B152,T760
V756: **A SYNTHETIC FIXTURE CALIBRATED ON *PEAK* DOES ⊥ TRANSFER — A DISTRIBUTION HAS A FLOOR & A SPREAD, & ONE SCALAR CARRIES NEITHER.** §T738: `BAND_REFERENCE_DB` fits a full strike onto real music's p99 ∴ LEVEL matches & **TAIL does ⊥** — the pattern's p01 EQUALS its median (a periodic beat returns to the same floor every bar) while music's p01 is far below. anything tuned to sit *just under the floor* of such a fixture sits **INSIDE THE TAIL** of the real thing, & the failure is **INVISIBLE OFFLINE ∵ OFFLINE RUNS THE FIXTURE**. ∴ when a fixture stands ∈ for a real signal, state WHICH MOMENTS of the distribution it reproduces & tune only against those; & a rest-state bias ! be fitted against a REAL floor, never a synthetic one|V477,V471,V713,T701,T707,T738
V757: **A FAMILY-WIDE TYPE MIGRATION ! LAND ∈ ONE COMMIT — THE FIRST MOVER REFUSES THE REST.** §T722 shipped a correctly-typed `data` depth port & **§V13 refused it into `displace.disp`**, ∵ every consumer ∈ the family (`displace.disp`, `remap.map`, `composite.mask`) is STILL declared `linear` & §T83's migration exists ONLY AS NOTES. ∴ **being RIGHT FIRST makes you INCOMPATIBLE**, & the type system correctly punishes it. when a migration spans a family, either move the whole family ∨ ship w/ the incumbent type & NAME the joint migration @ the site — never a lone correct port that ⊥ can connect to anything|V13,V205,V478,T83,T722,T768
V758: **AN OPTIONAL CAPABILITY ! COST NOTHING WHEN OFF — PRICE IT @ ALLOCATION, ⊥ @ USE.** §T722: a static 2nd texture port would have cost **~16 MB of rgba16float @ 1080p on EVERY render node** whether or ⊥ anyone wanted depth. `outputWhen` makes a conditional output **ALLOCATE NOTHING while its switch is off** — one line ∈ the compiler's allocation loop, & GENERIC ∴ the next optional output is free. corollary: an off-by-default switch ! be **PRICED ∈ ITS DESCRIPTION** (§T722 prices it *like a casting light*) so the user meets the cost at the moment of choosing|V585,V588,V721,T722
V759: **AN EXCEPTION STATED @ LANDING IS A PREDICTION, & ! BE TESTED LIKE ONE.** §T764 cleared 4 resource kinds & wrote that external textures need ⊥ clearing ∵ *the media pipeline re-registers per document* — an ASSUMPTION carried ∈ prose, ⊥ a gate. §B157 is the owner hitting exactly that clause **the same day**. ∴ when a fix declares something OUT of scope BY REASONING, either GATE the reasoning ∨ FILE it — an unexamined exception ∈ a commit message is a bug w/ a citation|V478,V620,V713,B157,T764
V760: **A FIXTURE'S OBSERVATION WINDOW ! COVER THE STRUCTURE IT MODELS — & a DISTRIBUTION'S SPREAD is what transfers, ⊥ its FLOOR.** §T766: `audioPattern` fails ⊥ ∵ its floor is too high but ∵ its **SPAN IS 3× TOO NARROW** on `high`, & the failure rate per band tracks the span mismatch EXACTLY. **& the floor cannot be lowered to fix it** — ∈ a log domain the constant sets the MEDIAN as much as the floor ∴ it TRANSLATES rather than WIDENS. real spread comes from SUSTAINED structure (a breakdown lasting seconds); brief dips are erased by a 0.04 s lag ∴ the fixture needs MULTI-BAR structure — **& the look gate renders 3.0 s = 1.4 bars ∴ it CANNOT SEE an 8-bar cycle**. ∴ a fixture & the gate that renders it are ONE INSTRUMENT: **lengthening the fixture w/o lengthening the window changes NOTHING THE GATE CAN OBSERVE**|V756,V471,V717,T701,T766 ⟶ **AMENDED — IT CUTS BOTH WAYS**: a window too SHORT to contain the structure observes nothing, **& a window LENGTHENED w/o an assertion that READS THE NEW REGION is PURE COST** — slower gates, ⊥ extra signal, & a false sense that the case is now covered. ∴ lengthen & ASSERT together, ∨ ⊥ at all (§T776 added a `phrase` row @ frame 440 for exactly this reason)
V761: **A COUNT FROM A TEXTUAL SCAN IS A GUESS — ENUMERATE BY EXECUTING THE REGISTRY.** §T776's blast radius was stated as **6** (§T701, stale), corrected to **10** by grepping `documents.ts`, & finally measured @ **9 of 37** by RUNNING the registry — the grep mis-attributed **E42 Current, which does ⊥ contain `audioPattern` at all**. a scan sees TEXT; a registry knows what is REGISTERED. ∴ ∀ claim of the form *N things are affected* ! come from the system's own enumeration, & a stale N ∈ a row is §V723's defect wearing a bigger number|V723,V478,V713,T701,T776
V762: **A RESOURCE KEYED BY *NAME* IS REUSED ACROSS MOUNTS ∴ A DOUBLE-MOUNT APPENDS WHERE YOU MEANT IT TO REPLACE — & CLEANUP ! REMOVE WHAT *ITS OWN* MOUNT ADDED.** §T774: `window.open` reuses by NAME (§V334) & StrictMode's mount→cleanup→mount left **2 root divs** ∈ one child document, the empty one filling a `100vh; overflow:hidden` body & **CLIPPING THE LIVE PANE OUT OF VIEW**. ⚑**the defect is INVISIBLE TO EVERY CHECK BELOW IT** — the pane mounted, the canvas configured, the pixels drew; the CONTAINER was off-screen. ∴ (a) ⊥ mount may assume a previous cleanup RAN; (b) a cleanup ! drop the node IT created, ⊥ *the* node; & (c) **when a thing renders correctly & is ⊥ SEEN, suspect the BOX before the PIXELS** — §V731's 5th reading (CSS, stacking, compositing) exists ∵ this class is unreachable from any probe pointed @ the content|V334,V731,V658,B51,T774,T739
V763: **CLEARING STATE & RESETTING THE CURSOR THAT GUARDS ITS RE-POPULATION ! BE ATOMIC — EITHER ALONE IS A NEW BUG.** §B157: clearing a media texture WITHOUT resetting `lastFrameId` leaves a still-registered webcam **PERMANENTLY BLACK** (the cursor says *already current*, so nothing re-uploads); resetting the cursor without clearing leaves the STALE PIXELS visible. **the pair heals; either half alone is worse than the original defect ∈ a different direction.** ∴ whenever a cache holds BOTH data & a freshness marker, they are ONE unit — & red-verify BOTH halves separately, which is how §B157 found it|V691,V759,V730,B157
V764: **⊥ EMPTY `catch` — a FAILURE THAT RENDERS IDENTICALLY TO A DESIGNED FALLBACK ! BE DISTINGUISHABLE BY A MESSAGE.** §B156's state C: `runOnce`'s `catch {}` swallowed EVERY inference rejection ∴ the node served mid-grey forever while acquisition reported `ready` — **pixel-for-pixel identical to the CORRECT ⊥-model state, w/ STRICTLY LESS SIGNAL**. the designed fallback is what makes the silence lethal: a fallback that looks like failure is fine ∵ it SAYS SO; a failure that looks like the fallback is **UNDIAGNOSABLE BY CONSTRUCTION**. ∴ wherever a graceful degradation exists, the FAILURE PATH INTO THE SAME PIXELS ! carry its own reason|V469,V731,V620,B156
V765: **AN EXAMPLE WHOSE STAR NODE CAN BE *UNAVAILABLE* ! GATE THE UNAVAILABLE STATE'S **NOTICE**, ⊥ ONLY THE AVAILABLE STATE'S PICTURE.** §T759 verified *both states* BY LOOKING AT PIXELS & shipped green; **neither state's NOTICE was gated** ∴ §B156. for such an example the ⊥-model path is ⊥ an edge case, it is **THE FIRST EXPERIENCE OF EVERY NEW USER** ∵ nothing downloads on its own. ∴ the gate ! assert WHAT THE USER IS TOLD, & the row ! LEAD W/ THE PICTURE (*this document is showing flat grey*) rather than name the dependency (*Depth needs …*), which reads as an optional extra|V471,V469,V764,T759,T385,T743
V766: **A TOLERANCE BAND ACCUMULATES DRIFT SILENTLY UP TO THE TOLERANCE — & the drift surfaces as a RIDER ON SOMEONE ELSE'S COMMIT.** §T776 regenerated `look-baselines.json` & found **E30-Nave `motion`/`range` & E43-Splice `f0max` had ALREADY MOVED**, all inside `max(10%, 0.003)` ∴ **never red, never noticed, & indistinguishable from the regenerating change's own effect** until the worker PROVED otherwise (`f0max` reads frame 0, which the arrangement cannot touch; reverting @ HEAD returns the new values). ∴ (a) ∀ regeneration ! SEPARATE *my change moved this* from *this had already drifted* & report both; (b) a tolerance is a LICENCE TO DRIFT that far, ⊥ a promise of stability — periodically regenerate & read the deltas even when nothing is red|V642,V643,V732,V713,T776
V767: **A METRIC SOME SUBJECTS HONESTLY FAIL ! BE A *RECORD*, ⊥ A *FLOOR*.** §T776's `phrase` row measures response to a musical breakdown; **Pasture & Splice score 0.048 ∵ they GENUINELY do ⊥ respond** — Pasture's medium outlasts a bar (§T778), Splice is quantised to beats. a FLOOR would condemn them for an honest property & pressure someone to fake a response to stay green. ∴ record the number, let it MOVE, & read it — a threshold is only honest where EVERY subject SHOULD clear it|V643,V471,V732,T776,T778
V768: **A SELF-SEEDED BASELINE CANNOT ENFORCE AN ABSOLUTE FLOOR — a RELATIVE band around a value MEASURED FROM THE SUBJECT can never catch that value SITTING JUST ABOVE the floor.** §T772: E44's `motion` baseline was **0.00311 against `LIVENESS_FLOOR` 0.002** — it cleared by 1.5× & PASSED ∴ **the gate that promises *every shipped example moves, and you can see it* HAD THE OWNER'S COMPLAINT RECORDED AS A PASSING NUMBER**. the baseline was measured from the same near-static picture it was meant to judge ∴ it could only confirm the example still looked the way it looked. distinct from §V765 (a state nobody gated); this is **a threshold a self-seeded baseline STRUCTURALLY CANNOT ENFORCE**. ∴ ∀ absolute floor ! be read against the POPULATION, ⊥ against each subject's own history — & values sitting within a small multiple of a floor are SUSPECTS, ⊥ passes|V643,V642,V766,V471,T772,T785
V769: **FRAME 0 IS THE THUMBNAIL — AN EXAMPLE ! OPEN ON ITS SUBJECT.** §T785 found **3 examples that open BEFORE THEIR SUBJECT EXISTS**: E24 (declared, `f0max` 0.0000), **E32 Pasture 0.0344 (1.7× `FIRST_FRAME_FLOOR`)** & **E41 Cinder 0.0687 (3.4×)** — Pasture opens on a dim speckle disc before the lace forms, Cinder on a featureless grey plate before the sparks. ⚑**the reasoning was ALREADY WRITTEN ∈ E24's OWN declaration — *a gallery thumbnail is frame 0* — & was NEVER GENERALISED**, which is how 2 more shipped. ∴ a simulation that needs N frames to become itself ! either **SEED its opening state** ∨ **START ITS CAPTURE LATER**, & `f0max` is the row that catches it — a declaration is for a DELIBERATE choice, ⊥ a way to retire a recurring defect|V768,V765,V471,T785,T757
V770: **A WHOLE-FRAME MEAN MEASURES CHURN, ⊥ COMPOSITION — & IT ERRS ∈ BOTH DIRECTIONS.** §T785: `motion` (mean |Δ| over the frame) **UNDER-reads a sparse bright subject on a large black field** — Nave, Gooeyball & Instanced Torus are all obviously alive & all sit ∈ the bottom third — & **OVER-reads a busy field whose COMPOSITION IS STATIC** — Obol churns @ 5.6× the floor while the emblem never moves, position, size & orientation identical across every frame. **Nave & Obol are ADJACENT ∈ THE RANKING & OPPOSITE ∈ FACT.** ∴ the metric is **a FILTER FOR WHERE TO LOOK, never the VERDICT** (§V471), & any *is it alive* question ! end @ a frame|V471,V643,V768,T785
V771: **A TEST BUDGET IS A HANG DETECTOR, ⊥ A PERFORMANCE GATE — & raising one LOSES NOTHING IF ⊥ GATE MEASURES DURATION.** §T781 corrected the orchestrator on exactly this: a budget that fires ONLY UNDER CONTENTION provides **⊥ slowdown signal** & costs **an ATTRIBUTION HUNT EVERY TIME IT FIRES** ∴ raising it **REMOVES A FALSE SIGNAL rather than losing a real one**. the honest objection is ⊥ *the timeout hides a slowdown* — it is *we have ⊥ instrument that would notice a slowdown*, which is a DIFFERENT gate (§T789). ∴ size budgets against the LOAD THE PROJECT ACTUALLY CREATES (measured 3.3× here), & if you want a performance signal, MEASURE DURATION on purpose|V620,V713,V491,T781,T789
V772: **⊥ WALL-CLOCK SLEEP MAY STAND ∈ FOR AN EVENT THAT HAS ITS OWN CLOCK — under load the 2 clocks do ⊥ slow TOGETHER.** §T781: `setTimeout(r, 120)` stood ∈ for *several preview ticks*, but the preview loop runs on **rAF** ∴ under contention a DIFFERENT NUMBER of ticks lands inside the fixed window & the test fails **AS AN ASSERTION, ⊥ A TIMEOUT** (`compiles: 3` vs `2`) — invisible to any budget. **⚑3RD INSTANCE of one shape**: §V491 (userEvent's inter-event delay), §T780/§T781 (budgets), & **46 sleeps across 32 files**. ∴ wait on the EVENT (a tick count, a flush, a deterministic clock), never on a DURATION that merely usually contains it|V491,V701,V737,T781,T788
V773: **2 ENTRY POINTS TO ONE CORE = A GATE THAT CANNOT SEE THE PRODUCT. GATE THE *PRODUCT'S* PATH, ∨ ENUMERATE THE DIFFERENCES & TEST EACH.** §T771: the app & the harness both *compile*, & differ ∈ **2 inputs** — the harness has **⊥ CHANNEL RESOLVER** (driven params degrade @ info; **the app halts on an error the harness cannot produce**) & the harness **does ⊥ FLATTEN** a component-free document (**the app ALWAYS flattens**). ∴ **935/935 green while 3 shipped examples were BLACK ∈ THE APP** — & one defect was a SINGLE DROPPED FIELD that broke EVERY VARIADIC PORT ∈ EVERY DOCUMENT. **⊥ amount of coverage on the wrong entry point substitutes for one run through the right one.** corollary (§V739's shape): `flatten.test.ts` pinned edges whose ALPHABETICAL order AGREED w/ their declared order — **a test where the correct & incorrect answers COINCIDE cannot fail**; the fixed pin CONTRADICTS them deliberately|V739,V755,V620,V713,T771,T790
V774: **A CAUSAL CLAIM IS A CLAIM ABOUT THE *STEADY STATE*, ⊥ ABOUT FRAME 0 — a WARM START does ⊥ weaken it if the seed is GONE by the time the claim is measured.** §T786: E9 Ember already made this trade (*the seeded generation is entirely replaced by births within about 4 seconds*) & it was never generalised, so E32 & E41 each re-derived the opposite & each shipped a black gallery card. **the test of compatibility is the GATE'S OWN FRAME**: Cinder asserts @ frame 132, *more than a TTL apart* ∴ a warm start is admissible BY ITS OWN REASONING. **⊥ admissible where the claim is testable @ EVERY frame** — Pasture's *turn the herd off & the frame stays empty forever* is genuinely broken by a seed ∴ that one needs a DIFFERENT fix, ⊥ a weaker claim|V769,V471,V725,T786,T793
V775: **CLOSING THE INSTANCES DOES ⊥ CLOSE THE CLASS — a swept defect RECURS BY COPY-PASTE UNLESS A GATE FORBIDS THE SHAPE.** §T535 measured `t4d: 0`, wrote it up & swept the catalogue; **5 MORE arrived AFTER the sweep — E40, E41, E42, E43, E44, CONSECUTIVE** — ∵ Wake introduced an understudy-bed pattern carrying it & **it was copy-pasted forward 4 times**. §B146/§T751 is the same lesson on a NODE TYPE; this is it on a PARAMETER. ∴ ∀ sweep ! end w/ a STRUCTURAL gate that rejects the shape, ⊥ a fixed list — & the cheapest gate is one w/ ⊥ render cost|B146,V478,T535,T751,T786
V776: **A CONTROL THAT LOOKS EDITABLE ! ACCEPT THE FIRST KEYSTROKE — `readOnly` + a `default: return` ∈ a keydown is SILENCE, & silence reads as BROKEN.** §B159: a focused number field swallowed every printable key ∵ text entry opened only on click, double-click ∨ `Enter`/`F2` ∴ the owner typed & **nothing happened & nothing said why**. ∴ (a) a printable key ! either DO something ∨ be visibly refused; (b) **the keystroke that STARTS an edit ! be the FIRST CHARACTER OF IT**, ⊥ discarded ∈ favour of the old value; (c) when a gesture-set is deliberately narrow, the field ! SAY the gesture (§V731's lesson: an unanswerable question becomes a stated line)|V53,V19,V731,V469,B159
V777: **A VALIDITY GATE ! BE VALUE-INDEPENDENT — ANSWER *EXTREMES*, ⊥ REPLAY A SIGNAL.** §T790's channel resolver returns **−1e6 / 0 / +1e6** rather than the audio that happened to be playing ∵ **whether a document COMPILES ! ⊥ depend on what the signal was doing at the compile instant** — that dependency IS §T771's defect (a compile landing on a 1.06 peak refused the whole plan). ∴ a replay-based gate catches ONE VALUE; an extremes-based gate catches **THE WHOLE CLASS** & cannot go quiet when the recording changes. general: where a property ! hold for ALL inputs, TEST THE BOUNDARIES, ⊥ a SAMPLE|V739,V773,V620,T790,T771
V778: **A TEST THAT BYPASSES THE INTERACTION CANNOT TEST THE INTERACTION — `fill()` NEVER PRESSES A KEY.** §B159 shipped w/ an e2e covering the very control it broke, ∵ Playwright's `fill()` **SETS THE VALUE DIRECTLY** & never produces a `keydown` ∴ a handler that swallows every printable key stayed green. same family as §V739 (a fixture that cannot exercise its subject) & §V773 (a gate on the wrong entry point). ∴ for a control whose BUG SURFACE IS INPUT HANDLING, the gate ! **PRESS KEYS** — & for a control whose bug surface is POINTER CLASSIFICATION, it ! move a real pointer a measured distance (§T796 pins 0 px, 2 px & 4 px separately)|V739,V773,V755,V620,B159,T796
V779: **A CLEAR ! BE PROVABLY A *RESET*, ⊥ A *DISCONNECTION* — assert that a NEW event STILL LANDS AFTERWARDS.** §T792 empties 2 accumulating diagnostic stores @ the document boundary; **the assertion that makes it trustworthy is ⊥ *the pane is empty* but *a warning emitted under the NEW document arrives*** — ∵ a fix that silently UNSUBSCRIBED would pass the first & fail the second, & would look identical to the user until the moment they needed a warning. same shape as §V763 (clear + cursor ! be atomic ∨ the webcam goes permanently black): **∀ clearing fix ! prove the thing still WORKS after, ⊥ only that it is EMPTY**|V763,V465,V722,V739,T792
V780: **AN UNIMPLEMENTED CONVENTION PEOPLE DESIGN AROUND IS WORSE THAN ⊥ CONVENTION.** §T794: *a gallery thumbnail is frame 0* was asserted ∈ ONE capture index & **NEVER IMPLEMENTED — there is ⊥ card image ∈ the codebase** — yet **3 examples (E24, E32, E41) each traded a readable opening for claim purity ∵ they believed it**, & E24 shipped a DECLARATION to pay for it. ∴ before designing around a stated convention, **CHECK IT EXISTS**; & a convention worth having ! be IMPLEMENTED ∨ DELETED, ⊥ left as prose that shapes decisions it cannot enforce|V478,V769,V775,T794
V781: **2 REMEDIES FOR 1 COMPLAINT MAY BE PRECONDITION & CONSEQUENCE, ⊥ INDEPENDENT HALVES — CHECK BEFORE TREATING THEM AS ALTERNATIVES.** §T797: the orchestrator read *react to darker colours* & *react to movement* as 2 halves that would COVER FOR EACH OTHER, reasoning that a dark scene has little luminance but plenty of motion. **MEASURED: FALSE — a frame difference of a dark picture is DARK BY THE SAME FACTOR** (E41's rig on the dark fixture: 0.0160 vs 0.0159, ⊥ visible change). exposure is the PRECONDITION; motion is what exposure then REVEALS. ∴ when a fix has 2 candidate halves, measure whether either works ALONE before shipping the cheaper one|V471,V732,T797
V782: **A SELECTOR PARAMETER ! JOIN THE IDENTITY KEY OF WHAT IT SELECTS — otherwise choosing writes a value NOTHING RE-READS.** §T810 added `device` to the media request key AT DESIGN TIME; w/o it the picker would have written a parameter the open effect never re-evaluates & the camera would never change. **§B157 is the SAME defect SHIPPED** — a media key w/ ⊥ document identity, found by the owner. 2 instances ∈ one day, one caught by a worker & one by the user ∴ the rule: **when you add a thing that CHOOSES, find the KEY that decides when the chosen thing is RE-OPENED, & put it ∈ there**|V691,V763,B157,T810
V783: **ENFORCE A MUST-⊥-PROMPT RULE *STRUCTURALLY*: give the test double ⊥ WAY to prompt.** §T810's `mediaDevices` mock **DEFINES ⊥ `getUserMedia` AT ALL** ∴ any code path that tries to open a camera THROWS ∈ the test rather than being caught by a reviewer's eye. **the fake's ABSENCE is the gate** — stronger than asserting *it was ⊥ called*, ∵ that assertion can be deleted while a MISSING METHOD cannot be un-missed. general: for *X ! never happen*, prefer a double where X IS IMPOSSIBLE over a spy that checks X did ⊥ occur|V721,V739,V733,T810
V784: **ROTATING A MONOTONE LOOKUP TABLE MAKES IT NON-MONOTONE — & a picture whose ONLY depth cue is TONAL ORDERING cannot survive that.** §T809 measured it on E27: the ramp is **monotone ∈ luminance BY DESIGN** (§T503 — near-black foot, white crest, ∴ colour CLIMBS WITH HEIGHT), & a phase rotation wraps the crest past white to stop 0 ∴ **black holes punched ∈ the summit, then the dome inverting to a silhouette**. the instant *brighter* stops meaning *further up the ramp*, **the relief loses its only reading — `phosphor1` is UNLIT ∴ there is ⊥ SHADING to fall back on**. ∴ palette CYCLING is ⊥ a free move on a relief; a CLAMPED SWEEP is, ∵ it preserves monotonicity. general: **before animating a mapping, ask what PROPERTY of it the picture is READING** — & if the answer is ORDER, ⊥ rotate it|V471,V147,T809,T503,T556
V786: **WHEN ONE MECHANISM IS PARAMETERISED 2 WAYS, THE KNOB YOU ADD ! BE *UNIT-FREE* — AN ABSOLUTE ADDITION SPLITS THE FAMILY.** §T814: `valueLag` (seconds) & `valueFilter` (hertz) are the SAME one-pole smoother ∴ an attack/release pair of ABSOLUTE times would have become **2 different parameters wearing one name**, & would have needed a SENTINEL default that steals a meaningful value. a **RATIO multiplies whatever time constant the node already computed** ∴ identical meaning ∈ both units & **a neutral value that is a real number, ⊥ a sentinel**. general: an addition to a family ! be expressible ∈ every member's OWN units, ∨ it is ⊥ an addition to the family|V478,V147,T814,T277
V787: **A NODE'S TITLE CONSTRAINS THE PARAMETERS IT CAN HONESTLY CARRY — if a knob makes the name FALSE, it belongs on a sibling.** §T814 gave the low/high-pass MODE to `valueFilter` & refused it to `valueLag`: **a Lag whose output is the RESIDUAL is ⊥ lagging anything**, & *low-pass/high-pass* are filter words on the node parameterised ∈ Hz. the capability is ⊥ lost — the residual is Lag → Math-subtract — the MODE merely SAVES that wiring where the name still fits. ∴ before adding a parameter ask **does the node's NAME still describe what it does @ every setting**|V478,V471,V786,T814
V788: **A VARIABLE COUNT CAN BE A *FIXED CAPACITY W/ DEGENERATE ELEMENTS* — & that is far cheaper than COUNTED-SET machinery.** §T817: Proximity allocates N×K links & **a link that does ⊥ exist collapses to ZERO LENGTH** (`position == tip`) ∴ zero area, free, invisible — ⊥ live-count buffer, ⊥ compaction pass, ⊥ indirect draw. **§T680 already does this ∈ E13's TIR slot**, so the trick was PROVEN & unnamed. ∴ before reaching for §T744's counted machinery, ask whether the absent element has a **HARMLESS DEGENERATE FORM** — a zero-length beam, a zero-alpha quad, a zero-radius point. the machinery is for when it does ⊥|V588,V754,T680,T744,T817
V789: **ASSERT AGAINST THE *STRUCTURE*, ⊥ AGAINST FRAME NUMBERS — a claim pinned to hardcoded frames goes STALE the moment the tempo, the fps ∨ the window changes.** §T819's phrase claim reads *the held value may change ONLY WHEN `floor(bar/4)` changes*, channel-exact over 1900 frames w/ **⊥ frame constant anywhere**, & is red-verified by pointing it at the MOVING bar channel. a frame-number version would have passed identically today & silently stopped meaning anything after any retempo — §V723's stale-count defect wearing a timeline. ∴ when a claim is ABOUT a structural quantity, **assert it AGAINST that quantity**; reserve frame constants for claims that are genuinely about a FRAME|V723,V681,V760,V732,T819,T749
