V790: **CONSTANT MAXIMUM READS AS CONSTANT — an example needs a FLOOR to have DYNAMICS, & *too busy* & *⊥ changing enough* are THE SAME COMPLAINT.** §T828: the owner reported E45 as hectic AND static ∈ one sentence. w/ every layer on @ full ∀ frame there is ⊥ reference for loud ∵ there is ⊥ quiet ∴ the eye reads ⊥ CHANGE, only DENSITY — & density does ⊥ evolve. **this is §V756's finding moved from the FIXTURE to the COMPOSITION**: a signal w/ ⊥ lower tail cannot express dynamics, & neither can a picture. ∴ (a) an example ! spend time NEAR ITS FLOOR, ⊥ only near its ceiling; (b) a CROSSFADE that never resolves shows BOTH shots FOREVER ∴ **a set CUTS by default & blends by exception**; (c) when a frame is *too busy*, the instrument is SUBTRACTION — ⊥ another layer, ⊥ more modulation|V756,V471,V767,T828,T776,T827
V791: **A `pointKernel` FED BY A `pointGenerator` IS A *STATELESS DISPLACEMENT MAP* — the generator RE-LAYS `position` EVERY FRAME & `firstRun` WRITES ARE DISCARDED.** §T827 proved it by teleporting every point to a 0.02 ball @ seed & getting **BYTE-IDENTICAL output**. ⚑**this EXPLAINS THE SHAPE OF BOTH FLAGSHIP EXAMPLES**: E31 & E35 have ⊥ lifecycle, ⊥ integration & ⊥ velocity, & smuggle audio ∈ through `radius`, **∵ THEY CANNOT HOLD STATE** — & it was written down NOWHERE. ∴ **a SIMULATION ! DROP THE GENERATOR & own its storage**; a generator+kernel pair can only DISPLACE. state it ∈ both node docs, ∵ the failure is SILENT — the seed appears to run & its effect vanishes|V588,V478,T827,T322
V792: **NEGATIVE SPACE ∈ AN *ADDITIVE* PROJECTION ! BE EXTRUDED ALONG THE VIEW AXIS — A PRISM, ⊥ A BALL. A VOID YOU CAN SEE THROUGH IS ⊥ A VOID.** §T827 reasoned a hollow additive shell would read as a bright RING w/ a dark middle; **the render refused it — 65k points on a thin shell project a SOLID DISC** ∵ the near & far caps BOTH land inside the silhouette & **additive blending has ⊥ DEPTH TEST** ∴ the hole fills w/ the shell's OWN far side. general: ∈ any accumulating-along-the-ray render, an ABSENCE only reads if it is absent **ALL THE WAY THROUGH**|V471,V627,T827,T828
V793: **A THRESHOLD ABOVE THE SIGNAL'S ACTUAL RANGE IS A PERMANENT OFF SWITCH — & ⊥ GATE CATCHES IT ∵ *ABSENCE IS ⊥ A FAILURE*.** §T828: E45's glitch subtracted **0.3** from an enveloped high band **that peaks @ 0.193** ∴ `amount` was ZERO EVERY FRAME & the effect NEVER APPEARED — for a whole example's life, through a full suite, ∵ **⊥ gate looked @ the glitch & a missing effect produces ⊥ failing pixels, only fewer of them**. it was found by the OWNER LOOKING AT A FRAME. ∴ (a) a threshold ! be checked against the driver's MEASURED range, ⊥ its nominal 0..1; (b) **∀ effect w/ an `amount` needs a gate that it FIRES**, ⊥ only the §V147 gate that it is ABSENT @ zero — an identity claim proves the OFF state & says NOTHING about the ON one|V147,V471,V756,V620,T828,T749
V794: **A COMMENT THAT *PREDICTS* CARRIES THE TASK ID IT WAITS ON — & landing that task DELETES it ∨ converts it to PAST-TENSE HISTORY ∈ THE SAME COMMIT.** *once §T-X lands*, a FAILING-GATE block left red, *when it lands*: each is a promise, & a promise w/o an id outlives everyone who remembers what it meant. §T805 found 3 species of the decay — a gate note on a test that now PASSES, a wish-note whose task LANDED, & a **DANGLING POINTER TO A NOTE ALREADY DELETED**. ⚑**& §T838 adds the 3rd OUTCOME the rule was missing: a note naming an UNBUILT thing that has ⊥ ROW ! CAUSE A ROW TO BE FILED.** *leave it, it is live* is correct ONLY when something OWNS it — otherwise it is a wish nobody owns (§V205's family). ∴ the 3 outcomes are **DELETE** (dead), **PAST-TENSE** (landed) & **FILE-THEN-CITE** (live but unowned). template: `shader-errors.spec`'s block, which is correct ∵ written as HISTORY|V205,V421,V478,V775,T805,T838
V795: **A SCRIPT THAT SPAWNS BACKGROUND LOAD ! GUARANTEE ITS OWN CLEANUP — a `kill` ON THE LAST LINE RUNS ONLY IF THE SCRIPT REACHES THE LAST LINE.** §T780/§T781's flake investigation created **ONE BUSY LOOP PER CORE** as deliberate synthetic contention & killed them @ the end; the script never got there ∴ **14 subshells ORPHANED TO PID 1 SPUN @ 75–97% CPU FOR 6h29m** until the OWNER noticed their machine. ⚠**& a plain `kill` DID ⊥ WORK — a tight `while :; do :; done` NEVER YIELDS ∴ SIGTERM is never serviced; it needs `-9`.** ∴ use `trap 'kill -9 $PIDS' EXIT INT TERM`, ∨ bound each child w/ `timeout`, ∨ both. ⚑**THE 2ND-ORDER COST IS THE REAL LESSON: an orphaned busy loop is INVISIBLE — ⊥ output, ⊥ window, ⊥ error — & it SILENTLY POISONS EVERY TIMING MEASUREMENT TAKEN AFTERWARDS, INCLUDING THE ONE IT WAS CREATED TO MAKE.** everything measured on this machine ∈ that window competed w/ 14 unaccounted burners → §T841|V713,V620,T780,T781
V796: **A GATE ! ⊥ RUN THROUGH THE MECHANISM THAT HIDES THE DEFECT.** §T826: 2 nodes shared a label & `nodeNames` **keeps the first-sorted id & silently drops the rest** ∴ a uniqueness check asked THROUGH `nodeNames` would see ONE name & PASS — the resolver's own tidiness IS the concealment. the gate counts collisions DIRECTLY ∈ the source. same family as §V739 (a fixture that cannot exercise its subject) & §V773 (a gate on the wrong entry point): **ask WHAT SWALLOWS THIS ERROR, & make sure the gate stands UPSTREAM of it**|V739,V773,V778,V782,T826
V797: **A RAW PER-FRAME DRIVE IS A DEFECT ONLY WHERE THE SLOT IS *CONTINUOUS* — where it SLAMS BY CONSTRUCTION, AN ENVELOPE ERASES THE PUNCTUATION.** §T842 found 8 raw lanes & only 2 were wrong: **`valueTrigger` IS the chop** (1 on the crossing frame, else 0), a `valueStep` bar-hold is MEANT to cut on the downbeat, & a `barPhase` ramp is already continuous. smoothing any of them would have destroyed the thing they exist to do. ⚑**∴ a class sweep ! judge each instance AGAINST ITS INTENT, ⊥ against the pattern that named the class** — a sweep that fixes every match is ⊥ closing a class, it is APPLYING A DEFECT UNIFORMLY. corollary from E43: a claim can survive a fix **BY CONSTRUCTION** — its slam clock lives ∈ the shader's own `floor(absTime·DEALS)`, ⊥ ∈ the driven amount, so smoothing the amount could ⊥ reach it|V775,V751,V471,V436,T842,T749
V798: **A MEASURED ARTIFACT A LATER TASK DEPENDS ON ! LIVE ∈ THE REPO — a scratchpad is GITIGNORED & an agent's transcript DIES W/ ITS SESSION.** §T821 measured a 7-node auto-level chain w/ specific constants, reported it ∈ prose, & left the script ∈ `scratchpad/t821/`. when §T843 came to BUILD it, **the session was gone & SPEC held only the SHAPE, ⊥ the NUMBERS** — the worker correctly REFUSED to re-derive blind (*re-deriving would be a different chain than the one you measured*), & only luck preserved the file. ∴ when a probe produces a RESULT a later row cites, **promote it: the numbers into the row, the script into `docs/`**. corollary: the worker's refusal was right — **a re-derived constant wearing a measured constant's authority is worse than an admitted gap**|V478,V713,V755,T821,T843
V799: **RED-VERIFY TELLS YOU *WHICH* PROPERTY YOUR GATE GUARDS — if it SURVIVES DETUNING the parameter it was built around, THAT PARAMETER IS ⊥ WHAT IT PINS.** §T843: the auto-level's range claim (→0..1) **survives detuning EITHER follower ratio** ∵ **a hit charges the 5 ms attack REGARDLESS of release** ∴ max always reaches 1. what the ratio actually moves is **THE SAG BETWEEN HITS — a SEPARATE claim**. ⚑2 consequences, both good: the gate is guarding the **AFFINE STRUCTURE** (multiply-instead-of-divide drops max to 0.06, red-verified) which is the STRONGER thing to pin; **& `Responsiveness` is SAFE to publish as a draggable knob ∵ a user CANNOT BREAK NORMALISATION w/ it, only change FEEL**. ∴ ⊥ assume a gate pins the knob it was written beside — MUTATE THE KNOB & find out|V666,V655,V739,V793,T843,T823
V800: **AWAITING A PROMISE IS ⊥ AWAITING THE WORK IT STARTS — await the chain that CLEARS THE GUARD, ⊥ the one that PRODUCES THE VALUE.** §B161: the harness awaited analyze's raw READBACK while the guard was cleared ∈ a later `.finally` ∴ the guard was still set @ the next sample & **every other frame was silently SKIPPED**. ⚑**& the observer effect rode on MICROTASK PHASE: a captured frame's extra `readOutput` injected microtasks that MOVED WHICH FRAMES GOT SAMPLED BY ONE** — so *measuring* changed *what was measured*, w/o touching the picture pipeline @ all (proved by holding brightness constant: zero divergence). ∴ (a) when a sampler has an in-flight guard, THE SETTLE CHAIN IS THE THING TO RETURN; (b) **a divergence that only appears UNDER FEEDBACK is a VALUE bug, ⊥ a RENDER bug** — separate them by freezing the picture; (c) fire-and-forget is correct LIVE (stale beats stalled) & wrong OFFLINE, & the same call can serve both by RETURNING the chain the live path ignores|V144,V701,V737,V713,B161
V801: **2 NODES COMPOSED THROUGH A SHARED COORDINATE CONVENTION ONLY AGREE WHEN THE GEOMETRY *MATCHES THE CONVENTION*.** §T830: `textureToAttribute` reads `position.xy` as CLIP [-1,1] → UV; `pointsFromTexture`'s grid sat @ 2.6×1.95 ∈ [-1.3,1.3] ∴ **the bridge squished the picture into the middle columns & smeared the edges — silently, ∵ both nodes were doing exactly what they promise**. setting the grid to **2×2 puts every point ON the clip square** ∴ colour is sampled @ **the very texel that set the point's height**. ∴ a composition is ⊥ correct merely ∵ the types match — **ask WHAT SPACE each side assumes**, & when a producer feeds a consumer that reads position AS an address, the producer's EXTENT is part of the contract|V427,V585,V734,V57c,T830
V802: **SCHEMA *ACCEPTANCE* IS ⊥ *SURVIVAL* — zod STRIPS unnamed keys SILENTLY, so *the schema accepts it* says NOTHING about what is ∈ the file after a ROUND TRIP.** §T848 measured it: `parameterDefinitionSchema` does ⊥ name `range` ∴ opening `AudioLevel.loom.json` reports `changed === false` & **saving DROPS `range:"floor"`** — the loader installs `parsed.data`, the STRIPPED copy. `component-sync` was asking the wrong question & passing. ∴ **for a CLOSED schema over a file we RE-WRITE, gate the ROUND TRIP (load → save → compare), ⊥ the parse** — & note the failure is INVISIBLE until a dropped declaration MATTERS, which is why it shipped|V88,V94,V478,T848,B111
V803: **THIS APP DEFAULTS TO SILENCE, & THE OWNER HAS PAID FOR IT 4× ∈ ONE SESSION. A TOGGLE ! SHOW ITS STATE, & A FEATURE W/ AN EMPTY RESULT ! SAY THE RESULT IS EMPTY.** the tally: §B159 a focused field swallowed every keystroke; §B156 an empty `catch` hid every inference failure behind a state that LOOKS designed; §B162 a refused fullscreen reported ⊥; §T859 a toggle w/ ⊥ checkmark over a document w/ nothing to draw. ⚑**the shared mechanism: ⊥ of these is an ERROR — each is a CORRECT-LOOKING NOTHING**, which is why ⊥ gate caught any of them & why the OWNER found all four. ∴ (a) ∀ toggle carries a STATE INDICATOR — the map already exists ∈ `menus/schemas.ts`, so an omission is the bug; (b) **∀ action whose result can legitimately be EMPTY ! DISTINGUISH *empty* FROM *did ⊥ run***; (c) §V793's rule generalised — an identity/off claim says NOTHING about the ON state, & the ON state is where the user lives|V793,V764,V731,V469,B159,B156,B162,T859
V804: **A VALUE THAT WILL BE RE-TUNED SHIPS AS AN *ALIAS*, ⊥ A LITERAL — so the next round RE-POINTS ONE NAME instead of GUESSING A NEW VALUE.** §T853: the owner had rejected this band 3× (*ever so slightly too bright*, *dial down a tiny little bit more*, *dividers LIGHTER*) ∴ round 4 shipped `--bg-header: var(--bg-void)` — a NAME pointing @ a RUNG — & **its gate pins the BOUNDS & the DIRECTION, ⊥ WHICH RUNG** ∴ round 5 is a 1-line re-point that CANNOT leave the ladder, ⊥ a 4th hex nobody can argue about. corollary: **arguing from the SCALE beats arguing from taste** — the steps up measured +5.43 & +6.90 against a *tiny* ask & a 4.0 structural floor, which is why DOWN was the only move on the scale @ all|V478,V767,T853,T712
V805: **AN EXAMPLE WHOSE PARAMETERS ARE FROZEN ∈ CODE CANNOT BE ITERATED — & A FEEDBACK LOOP THAT WILL ⊥ CONVERGE IS THE DIAGNOSTIC.** §T880: E46 took **4 ROUNDS** of *still too X* & the cause was ⊥ taste — **one scalar was exposed & colours, speeds, glow falloff & shadow softness were ALL ∈ WGSL** ∴ every round meant EDITING A SHADER & RE-RENDERING rather than TURNING A KNOB. ∴ when feedback keeps missing, **ask what is ADJUSTABLE before asking what is WRONG**; & a node that hides its author's own parameters is powerful & UNCONTROLLABLE, which is §T868's finding (*you ! write WGSL to touch a point*) one layer up. corollary: **the number of exposed parameters is a property of the EXAMPLE, ⊥ only of the node** — an example that publishes nothing cannot be dialled by anyone, incl its author|V471,V732,T868,T880,T827
V806: **AN AUTO/MANUAL BLEND ! HAND OVER ON *ACTIVITY*, ⊥ ON PRESENCE — & THE HAND-BACK IS AN ENVELOPE, ⊥ A TIMEOUT.** §T857: the pointer's `hand` is its own SPEED (squared for sign), through a fast-attack/slow-release envelope ∴ **a cursor that has never moved reads EXACTLY 0 & the default frame is BIT-IDENTICAL**, a decisive gesture holds control LONGER than a nudge, & control returns SMOOTHLY rather than snapping @ a fixed delay. ∴ ⊥ blend auto & manual by SUMMING (the auto term dominates & the manual one rides a jump — the defect §T857 fixed) & ⊥ by a boolean (which snaps); **mix by an AUTHORITY the user's own gesture produces**. corollary: **widen ONLY the manual scale** — the auto term's recorded meaning then survives untouched, & every measurement pinned to it keeps its meaning|V751,V735,T857,T842,T814
V807: **A `position: sticky` CHILD STICKS TO THE SCROLLPORT — WHICH IS THE SCROLL CONTAINER'S ***PADDING BOX***, ⊥ ITS VISIBLE TOP.** §T876: `.list` had 4px of top padding ∴ **every sticky header ∈ every pane parked 4px LOW, & that 4px band was LIVE** — content scrolled up through it & was clipped by the BORDER box rather than hidden by the header, which reads exactly like a misaligned header. ∴ **a scroll container that holds sticky children ! have ⊥ TOP PADDING**; put the spacing on the first child, where it SCROLLS AWAY like the content it spaces. ⚠**& the orchestrator's *take the search out of the scroller* was a fragile fix for a problem that did ⊥ exist — the search was already a SIBLING**: when a symptom looks like an offset bug, MEASURE THE BOX MODEL before moving the DOM|V471,V478,T876
V808: **A GREP-BASED GATE PROVES A STRING EXISTS, ⊥ THAT IT IS ∈ THE RIGHT PLACE — PRESENCE IS ⊥ STRUCTURE.** 2 instances found together: **§T885** — `doc-drift` reads only `name(type)` claims ∴ an UNTYPED `┄ tearb1` annotation naming a RENAMED node is unreachable BY DESIGN, & one rename left 2 stale claims of which the gate saw 1; **§T886** — `readme.test.ts` greps for a link ANYWHERE ∈ the file ∴ **it could ⊥ see that a blank line had ENDED THE MARKDOWN TABLE & dropped a row OUT of the index** while the link still matched. ⚑**the precision that makes such a gate cheap is the SAME PROPERTY that blinds it** ∴ when a gate is scoped by a PATTERN, ask what the pattern CANNOT SEE & decide whether that blind spot is ACCEPTABLE — & say so ∈ the gate, so the next reader inherits the limit rather than the false confidence|V796,V739,V773,V620,T885,T886
V809: **CHECK THE THING, ⊥ THE EXPLANATION OF THE THING — & a COMMENT THAT NAMES A DEPENDENCY ROTS THE MOMENT THE DEPENDENCY MOVES.** §T888's worker named the pair itself: **§T876 was A WRONG DIAGNOSIS OF A REAL BUG** (the search box was already outside the scroller; the padding box was the culprit) & **§T888 was A REAL REGRESSION HIDDEN BY A COMMENT DESCRIBING THE OLD PRIMITIVE** (*it inherits the tooltip's 280px cap*, false the moment the card became a popover). ⚑**both are *the explanation was checked instead of the thing*** — & the 2nd is sharper ∵ **the stale comment survived exactly ONE COMMIT before the owner hit it**, which is how fast an explanation can outlive its subject. ∴ when a value is inherited from ANOTHER component, the comment naming that component is a DEPENDENCY & ! be re-read when the component changes; better, DERIVE the value (§T888 computes the cap from the asset's own IHDR) so there is nothing to describe|V807,V780,V794,T876,T888
V810: **WHEN A KNOB FINALLY EXISTS, THE OWNER TURNS IT FIRST — a component's PUBLISHED DEFAULTS ARE THE SHIPPED LOOK, & choosing them for the owner re-freezes what the knob was built to unfreeze.** §T880 gave E46 live controls after §V805 diagnosed that 4 feedback rounds failed ∵ nothing was adjustable. ∴ the component wrapping WAITS: the owner dials, & the values they land on become the published defaults. ⚑**the general rule: a capability built to END a feedback loop ! be HANDED to the person who was giving the feedback, BEFORE anything downstream bakes a value ∈** — otherwise the loop restarts one layer up, w/ the same *still ⊥ quite* & the same absence of a lever|V805,V471,T880,T889,T843
V811: **`driven` IS BOTH A MODE NAME & A COMPUTED FLAG ∈ OUR OWN RESOLVER, & THAT OVERLOAD IS WHY THE MODE MODEL READS AS REDUNDANT.** `resolve.ts` returns `{mode:"bind", source:"driven", driven:true}` — a **bind** reports itself as driven, & so does an expression that references anything. ∴ *driven* names (a) one of 5 authored modes & (b) the DERIVED property *this value comes from elsewhere*, which every non-static mode has. ⚑**the owner read (b) & asked why (a) exists — the question is CORRECT & the answer is that we overloaded the word.** the 3 scalar modes are ⊥ 3 kinds of thing: **`expression` = our grammar, ? reference anything; `driven` = a bare CHANNEL NAME ∴ a DEGENERATE EXPRESSION; `bind` = a sibling|scope ref, READ-ONLY — ⊥ TD's Bind, which is TWO-WAY.** ∴ our `bind` is a one-term expression w/ a misleading name, & the owner's expectation (bind ⇒ persistent 2-way, everything else ⇒ 1-way read) is **the TD-correct one we did ⊥ build**. ⊥ collapse anything on this basis alone: `driven` is LIVE (21 shipped uses, `resolve.ts:623`) & its docblock's *"declared-but-reserved until Phase 2"* is STALE — I checked before claiming a gap (§T840's lesson). the actionable half is NAMING & the badge, ⊥ deletion|V108,V107,V113,T840,T895 ⟶ **OWNER RULED: DELETE THE MODE (§T897).** I flagged *⊥ collapse on this basis alone*; they reaffirmed twice ∴ that is the decision & it is taken. the naming half I offered was the SMALLER half — a mode that duplicates another mode's reach costs **a switch under EVERY parameter**, & that cost is paid ∀ parameters ∀ nodes forever, ⊥ only where the mode is used|
V812: **A NEW CONTROL SURFACE IS ⊥ VERIFIED BY RENDERING — IT ! BE *MOVED*, & THE ORCHESTRATOR ANNOUNCING IT ! BE THE ONE WHO MOVES IT.** §B166: §T880's reflected controls rendered w/ correct values & refused every write ∵ read paths took the effective schema & write paths took the static one. **A read-only bug ∈ an editor is INVISIBLE to every gate that asks *does it appear?*** — & it was invisible to me ∵ I relayed *the knobs exist, go turn them* from a summary, having turned NONE. ∴ ⚑**∀ capability whose WHOLE POINT is that the owner can now change something, the acceptance test is A ROUND TRIP THROUGH THE REAL BUS — write, persist, read back — ⊥ a screenshot & ⊥ a builder's report.** the §T840 rule (⊥ file a gap from a summary) generalises: ⊥ ANNOUNCE a capability from a summary either. the cost lands on the OWNER, who tries the thing you promised & finds it dead|B166,T880,T840,V805,V810
V813: **A PERSISTENCE KEY IS AN ADDRESS, ⊥ A NAME — A PRODUCT RENAME ⊥ REACH IT.** §T899 renamed Shaderloom→Loom; the 11 localStorage/IndexedDB/CacheStorage/Symbol keys KEPT the old prefix ON PURPOSE. changing one is ⊥ a rename but a **SILENT MIGRATION**: the app looks up a key that has never existed, finds nothing, & reports an empty state that is INDISTINGUISHABLE from a new install ∴ **the user's autosaved projects, pane layout & keybindings are ⊥ deleted — they are ORPHANED, which is worse ∵ ⊥ error fires.** the benefit is ZERO: ⊥ user ever reads a storage key. ∴ ⚑**rename what is SEEN; ⊥ what is ADDRESSED.** where the 2 disagree, a COMMENT reconciles them — the confusion of a future reader is cheap, & data is ⊥. (if a key ! move later it moves W/ a read-old-fallback, the `LEGACY_*` pattern already ∈ `layout-storage.ts` — ⊥ as a side effect of a find-&-replace)|T899,V108
V814: **"I READ ALL N PATHS" IS A LIST YOU HAPPENED TO FIND, ⊥ A PROOF THE LIST IS CLOSED.** §T895: I enumerated 4 parameter write paths, verified each preserves `...slot.bindings`, & concluded the domain was innocent & the UI guilty. **THE 5TH PATH — `apply-patch`'s `setParameters` — WAS ∈ A FILE I NEVER OPENED, & IT WAS THE BUG.** the enumeration was correct & the CLOSURE CLAIM was ⊥. ∴ ⚑**an exhaustive claim ! come from a MECHANISM that makes exhaustiveness true — a type that ⊥ compile w/ an unhandled case, a grep whose pattern IS the definition of the set, a single funnel every write ! pass — ⊥ from having looked in the places one thought of.** the honest form of the finding was *these 4 preserve; I do ⊥ know they are all* — which would have sent the reproduction to the bus FIRST, where it landed ∈ one step. related: §V808 (classification ⊥ coverage) is this same error one level down|T895,V808,T840,V812
V815: **A MIGRATION IS ⊥ DONE WHEN THE VALUES MATCH — IT ! PRESERVE THE DIAGNOSTIC TIER TOO.** §T897 moved 145 `driven` bindings to `.chan` expressions BYTE-EQUAL ∈ value & still reddened ~30 examples: the old form degraded to **INFO** when ⊥ channels resolver was present, the new one to **WARNING**. ∴ **a gate reading *compiles w/ no diagnostics* is a REAL contract & a tier change BREAKS it while every value stays right.** ⚑the general shape: **an equivalence proof ! cover EVERY observable the system exposes — value, TIER, MESSAGE, timing — ⊥ only the one the migration was about.** §V814's closure problem again, one axis over: *I checked the values* is a list of what you thought to check. corollary that saved this one: **the failing gate was found BEFORE the commit ∵ the migration was run against the full suite, ⊥ against its own new tests**|V814,T897,T901,V338
V816: **CHROME THAT ! BE LEGIBLE OVER A LIVE PREVIEW TILE CANNOT BE RENDERED BY A NODE — it belongs to the node HEADER ∨ to the PANE-LEVEL chrome layer, NEVER to the preview slot.** §T892: nodes live inside a transformed `.react-flow__viewport` ∴ a stacking context; the preview surface composites full-pane @ z30 over each slot rect ∴ **anything a node draws inside its own slot is occluded PRECISELY WHEN THE PREVIEW IS RUNNING** — & ∵ that surface is `pointer-events:none`, **EVERY DOM TEST STILL FINDS THE BUTTON, CLICKS IT & PASSES** (§T675 found this the first time; §T892 is the second). ∴ ⚑**a *put it on the preview* request is ALWAYS a layer question, ⊥ a CSS-position question — & the class of bug it hides is INVISIBLE TO THE DOM, ONLY VISIBLE ON SCREEN.** the seam that makes it cheap now exists (`--z-canvas-chrome: 31`, positioned by the same `slotScreenRect` the compositor uses) ∴ the NEXT such control is a small change, ⊥ an investigation|T892,T675,V112,V111
V817: **∈ A SHARED TREE THE GIT *INDEX* IS SHARED STATE — `git commit` ! CARRY EXPLICIT PATHSPECS, ALWAYS.** §T897's slice 1 swept 3 of §T900's files ∵ another agent had them STAGED; slice 2 used explicit paths & was clean. ⚑**BUT THE REAL FINDING IS ⊥ THE LEAK: THE AUTHOR'S OWN GUARD PRINTED `LEAK` & THE CHAIN WAS ALLOWED TO CONTINUE.** ∴ **a warning that fires & is overridden is WORSE than no warning** — it costs the same to build, teaches the operator that firing means *proceed*, & will be ignored FASTER next time. a guard worth having ! STOP the thing it guards ∨ ⊥ exist. (the leak itself was harmless — additive base of §T900's own arc, which that agent then completed ∈ `2af2c42` — but the history now shows one agent's files under another's message, which is why it ! be TOLD, ⊥ quietly absorbed)|V814,T897,T900,V753 ⟶ **2ND INSTANCE, SAME HOUR**: §T903's funnel edit (`resolve.ts`'s widened `ParameterSchemaSource` + `parameter-commands.ts`'s setMode fix) also landed ∈ `b0a4d2e` ∴ **2 agents' work is now filed under a 3rd's message.** the code is right; the HISTORY is ⊥. ∴ this is ⊥ a slip, it is the **DEFAULT OUTCOME of concurrent agents sharing one index** — ∴ pathspecs ! be a HABIT ⊥ a correction|T903,T900
V818: **A DECLARATION SITE ! CARRY *ALL* OF WHAT THE THING IS — ⊥ THE HALF THE HOST LANGUAGE HAPPENS TO EXPRESS.** §T900 rejected moving point attributes into the kernel's `struct Point`: a WGSL field gives NAME & TYPE, while an attribute is `{name,type,semantic,qualifier,default}`. **the missing 3 do ⊥ vanish — they get SMUGGLED BACK as convention** (a field called `tint` MEANS colour), which is index-as-identity wearing a new hat, & the codebase already banned it BY NAME. ⚑**& the tell that the move was wrong was available WITHOUT the analysis: the generator ALREADY REWRITES `Point` (injecting `alive`/`spawnCount`) ∴ the *one source of truth* the move promised WAS ALREADY IMPOSSIBLE.** ∴ before unifying 2 declarations, ask what EACH carries that the other cannot, & whether either is already rewritten downstream. corollary (§B168): a check that RE-DERIVES what it guards is a 2nd implementation free to disagree — guard the ARTEFACT, ⊥ a recomputation of it|T900,V720,V588,B168,T867
V819: **A CLOSURE MECHANISM ! KEY ON *SHAPE*, ⊥ ON A NAME — & §V814 BIT THE VERY TOOL BUILT TO ENFORCE §V814.** §T903's 1st census keyed on the type NAME `NodeDefinition`, reported a confident 102 reads, & **SILENTLY MISSED `pulse.ts`** — which declares its OWN structural `SchemaSource { get: (type) => { parameters: ParameterSchema } }` & was reading a placed node's schema statically. ∴ **a name-based closure gate would have SHIPPED WITH A HOLE, while asserting there were none** — the worst possible outcome, ∵ the next §B167 would arrive w/ a green gate standing over it. fixed by making the funnel's argument STRUCTURAL & keying the census on SHAPE. ⚑**the rule: an exhaustiveness tool ! be checked against a consumer that DELIBERATELY does ⊥ name the thing** — & deliberate over-inclusion is correct here, ∵ **over-inclusion costs a ledger line & under-inclusion costs a 4th silent surface.** (⊥ a lint rule ∵ eslint is ⊥ type-aware here & `node.parameters` vs `definition.parameters` are SYNTACTICALLY IDENTICAL — the distinguishing fact IS a type ∴ the tool ! be a checker)|V814,V458,T903,B167
V679: **a MODULE-LEVEL holder + IDEMPOTENT registration = ORPHANED ON HMR, silently & permanently.** T709 measured it: double-click → browser opens (count 1); `touch` the command module → Vite pushes ONE HMR update; double-click → **count 0, empty console, ⊥ exception, dead until a HARD RELOAD.** re-executing the module mints a SECOND `WeakMap` & a second EMPTY holder, while the handler ALREADY registered on the bus (registration is idempotent, the bus has ⊥ unregister) keeps reading the FIRST ∴ the canvas writes holder #2 & the command reads `null` from holder #1 & refuses w/ a `severity:"info"` diagnostic NOTHING DISPLAYS. touching the CANVAS ∨ the VIEW did ⊥ do it — only the command module's OWN re-execution. ∴ hold cross-module singletons on a `Symbol.for` registry (`src/domain/commands/command-holder.ts`), ⊥ a module-local map — **§V483 recorded this shape ONCE (webmcp.ts) & the code did ⊥ follow it**|V483,V78,T709
V680: **1 dead COMMAND is N dead AFFORDANCES — & that symptom shape is a DIAGNOSTIC.** §V78 routes every door through the keymap ∴ T709's 3 entry points (double-click, `tab`, the canvas menu row) name ONE command ∴ the owner's *"neither… nor… or anything really"* was the strongest evidence it was the COMMAND & ⊥ either gesture. ∴ when several unrelated affordances die TOGETHER, look at what they SHARE before looking at any of them|V78,V679,T709
V475: `displace` reads x from RED & y from GREEN ∴ a MONOCHROME field moves every pixel along the SAME 45° diagonal — that is a SHEAR, ⊥ a lens. `mono` ! be OFF on a displacement source (T507)
V476: ⊥ ship an example whose first frame ASKS FOR A PERMISSION. the first `audioIn` ∈ a graph opens the device ON LOAD ∴ a mic branch ∈ a gallery example is an ambush. E24 ships pattern|file & deliberately ⊥ a 3rd mic branch (T504,V329) **& its INVERSE for `mouse` (T651): the pointer is the live device nobody thinks of BECAUSE it costs nothing to open — ⊥ prompt, ⊥ hardware, ⊥ moment where anyone is asked — so it enters documents UNEXAMINED exactly where `audioIn`'s ambush cannot.**
V444: **a schema that ACCEPTS ON WRITE & REFUSES ON READ persists UNLOADABLE state.** B100: `map` mode saved & then would ⊥ open — the document was destroyed by being SAVED CORRECTLY. ∴ the write path & the read path ! be the SAME schema, & the gate is a ROUND TRIP (write it, read it back) rather than either direction alone.
V445: **DERIVE-⊥-EXTEND, done properly, means there is NOTHING SEPARATE TO FORGET.** T485 did ⊥ copy a 4th clause into `countNodeNameReferences`; each reference kind became ONE function asked EITHER question (a null rename counts, a string rewrites), & both public surfaces iterate the same clause list. "a 5th kind cannot teach the rename & forget the count because there is nothing separate to forget" is the test of whether a derivation is real: if you can still describe a way to update one & miss the other, it is ⊥ derived.
V446: a GATE ! consult the SAME TRUTH SOURCES its neighbours do, ⊥ invent a 4th. T486's new middle link first flagged `ui.beginRename`, which is LIVE via mounted-canvas registration — so it now checks bus ∪ APP_REGISTERED ∪ PLANNED_COMMANDS, the same 3 the planned-commands check already used. a gate w/ its own private notion of "registered" produces false reds that teach people to ignore gates.
V441: **StrictMode's mount→cleanup→remount disposes a `[]`-memo SINGLETON that never rebuilds.** B96: the value-history store is created by a `useMemo([])` & disposed ∈ an effect cleanup ∴ the probe-cleanup ran while the memo did ⊥, & the ONE instance the app holds latched `disposed` ∀ the whole session. pushes still landed ∈ the rings; `schedule()` silently refused; ⊥ subscriber ever fired; a click's re-render — a DIRECT SNAPSHOT READ — was the only thing that updated. B51's family a 2nd time. ∴ a disposer paired w/ a `[]` memo ! be IDEMPOTENT & REVIVABLE, | the memo ! share the effect's deps.
V442: **⊥ diagnose loop-liveness on a WARM HMR session.** a long-lived Vite HMR session can end up running TWO instances of vgpu's module graph whose frame loops STARVE each other — a dead transport that looks exactly like B81 & VANISHES on a cold `--force` server. cost half of B96's hunt. 3rd decoy ∀ V434's list, beside occlusion throttling & the inspector's zero-frame display.
V443: **a TEXT-SCANNING gate reads COMMENTS too.** the V17 literal-colour gate failed on a hex I quoted ∈ a CSS comment explaining a token change. ⊥ a false positive worth suppressing — naming the TOKEN is better prose anyway — but worth knowing before someone "fixes" the gate to skip comments & loses the case where a literal hides ∈ one.
V439: **a GLYPH is sized to its BUTTON, ⊥ to the label scale around it.** the transport's reset & loop & the viewer's fullscreen all rendered @ `--fs-meta` (11px) inside a `--control-h` control & read as SPECKS — the owner reported all 3. text inherits the surrounding scale correctly; a glyph is an ICON wearing a font & wants the control's size.
V440: **2 glyphs ! differ ∈ SILHOUETTE, ⊥ merely ∈ shape.** `⠿` (grip) & `⊞` (boxed plus) are both DENSE SQUARES ∴ they read as the same button @ 11px & the owner said so immediately. thin arrows `⇄` against a striped block `▤` are told apart WITHOUT BEING READ — which is the actual job of an icon ∈ a tight row.
V438: **SEPARATE BY TONE, ⊥ BY A DRAWN LINE — & an INSET RING on a box that already has a BORDER draws a SECOND EDGE.** my `2096a6f` added `box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px` to a preview slot that already had `border-bottom`, ∴ a visible doubled line the owner reported. the same commit LIFTED `--bg-void`, which made the ring redundant: body `--bg-raise` → slot `--bg-sunken` → black tile is 3 TONE STEPS & needs ⊥ lines @ all. SWEPT the whole stylesheet set: exactly 1 other rule combines an inset shadow w/ a border (`.hint`, a keycap w/ a 2% top bevel) & it is INTENTIONAL. ∴ a 1px inset RING is the smell; a soft directional bevel is ⊥.
V437: **a REQUIREMENT delivered SITE-BY-SITE is ⊥ delivered.** the owner asked ∀ a PROPERTY — "abstime should be something that keeps growing and does not get set... right now time and frame both reset at the end" — & I shipped it as a FEATURE @ 1 site per round: expressions (T461), then texture shaders (T468, filed as a FINDING that it reached only expressions), then point kernels (B97, filed as a NEW BUG). **3 rounds, & the SAME gap each time, because I checked SITES off instead of verifying the PROPERTY.** & the LFO (B98) was a 4th site I never looked at.
 ∴ when an owner states a PROPERTY, ENUMERATE EVERY PLACE IT ! HOLD BEFORE BUILDING, & GATE THE PROPERTY — ⊥ the sites. the gate here is: **⊥ clock-reading surface may see a value that decreases across a loop boundary**, asserted over every surface that can read a clock, so site #5 fails ⊥ ships.
V436: **A NODE'S CLOCK IS A DESIGN DECISION, ⊥ a default.** FREE-RUNNING (an LFO, a noise phase, anything the author thinks of as "always going") reads the ABSOLUTE clock ∴ a timeline loop cannot touch it. TIMELINE-ANCHORED (a Timer, a scrubbed envelope, anything whose position ∈ the piece is the point) reads the timeline clock ∴ it wraps BY DESIGN. shipping every value node on the timeline clock made the 1st kind unusable ∈ a loop & ⊥ one noticed until a real project looped.

V433: **2 COMPILES ! DERIVE THEIR SCOPE FROM 1 SOURCE, | they agree until they do ⊥.** B95's real mechanism (⊥ the enumeration I guessed): the per-frame `animate()` compile OMITTED the sink argument & fell back to every-visible-node, while the base plan compiles against the preview SCHEDULER's kept set. IDENTICAL on a fresh load — animation works, every unit suite green — & they DIVERGE the moment the scheduler suspends 1 offscreen preview: base loses a pass, `isUniformOnlyChange` goes false, push returns null, & **EVERY DRIVEN PARAMETER ∈ THE PROJECT HOLDS ITS LAST VALUE ∀ THE REST OF THE SESSION**, w/ 1 `structuralDrift` warning as the only witness. V222's silent-rider verbatim. cure: `animate` reads the SAME `scheduledPreviews` store the structural compile subscribes to ∴ the 2 CANNOT disagree about what exists.
V434: **NAME THE RED HERRINGS ∈ THE COMMIT.** B95 cost half a hunt to 3 things that LOOK like the bug: a WEDGED RENDERER (CDP eval timeouts), **macOS OCCLUSION THROTTLING (~2fps rAF ∴ live play LOOKS FROZEN while entirely correct)**, & the inspector showing the ZERO-FRAME throwaway resolution BY DESIGN (so "Value 1 reads 0.00" is ⊥ evidence of anything). I reasoned confidently from that 3rd one & was wrong. ∴ when a hunt is long, the DISCARDED hypotheses are part of the finding — the next hunter meets the same 3 decoys.
V431: **a HOST that THROWS on re-registration turns a CAPTURE bug into a PERMANENT one.** B93's mechanism, measured: this Chrome exposes `registerTool` & **⊥ `provideContext`**, & `registerTool` THROWS `InvalidStateError: Duplicate tool name` — 140× ∈ the reproduction tab. so the app's first publication (captured while `backend === undefined` ∴ ports `{}`) was kept ∀ THE LIFE OF THE PAGE, & the throw ALSO aborted the connected-row publish. ∴ a transport ! read its surface as a PROVIDER @ call time, never capture it — B76's cure, applied where the old comment explicitly said publication "must happen once per surface". tolerate the DUPLICATE error specifically; keep every other one LOUD.
V432: **ABSENCE-BY-TRANSPORT is ⊥ always a bug — say WHICH it is.** B93 was 2 different things: in-page was a CAPTURE bug (fixed), while stdio genuinely lacks `compile_project`/`play`/`get_diagnostics` because they require app-side sources a headless server has ⊥ (⊥ frame loop, ⊥ compile command). the designed cure is T451's ATTACHMENT — while a tab is attached, `serve.ts` answers `tools/list` & `tools/call` from the TAB's surface ∴ attaching gives the UNION. ∴ distinguish CAPTURED-WRONG from HONESTLY-ABSENT before fixing, & gate the union rather than the parity.
V429: **a MISS carries REAL DATA, ⊥ a SENTINEL.** T483's ray miss publishes the ray END and the FULL distance rather than a magic value — so downstream arithmetic on `hitPosition` is meaningful ∈ both cases & ⊥ one can accidentally treat a sentinel as a position. a sentinel is a value that LOOKS like data & is ⊥, which is the plausible-wrong class expressed as a number.
V430: **run the SRC-WIDE suite before committing, ⊥ the suites you have been running.** T481/T482's fallout — an env wire breaking node-view's no-sockets pin, & T477's field row reopening E25's overlap gutter — were HONEST REDS outside the author's working set. same mistake as B78, where I shipped a global token change having run only 2 local suites. the suite nearest your edit is ⊥ the suite that owns what you changed.
V427: **"both are textures" is ⊥ "both survive the same bridge".** D1 was abandoned after 9 device passes: `textureToAttribute` reads w/ `textureLoad` — NEAREST, UNFILTERED — & a reaction-diffusion is a NEAR-BINARY field. a displaced SURFACE is then trapped: mesh coarser than the field → a front narrower than the vertex spacing falls between vertices & renders as SPIKES; mesh finer → every vertex ∈ 1 texel shares a height & the surface STEPS. the only stable point is 1 vertex per texel, which forces a rectangular sheet & makes the field's feature size the FRAME's feature size — a bas-relief of a 2D picture, ⊥ a crossing. **E20 survives the SAME bridge ONLY because NOISE IS SMOOTH AT EVERY SCALE. a simulation is ⊥.** the pitch assumed they were interchangeable; that was the error. TRANSFERABLE: E27 works on the same bridge because **POINTS HAVE ⊥ SHARED EDGE to tear or facet**.
V428: **a VARIADIC order that falls back to an ID SORT is a SILENT BEHAVIOURAL CHOICE.** E27's first build opened on a BLACK WEBCAM: V131 puts a variadic port's order on the EDGES, none were declared, & the id tiebreak put `e-cam-pick` first — V363's exact null state selected by SPELLING. ∴ where order MEANS something, DECLARE it & assert that the ids would sort the other way, so the test proves the declaration is load-bearing.
V425: **ANALYTIC ∈ CONNECTIVITY ≠ ANALYTIC ∈ GEOMETRY, & I conflated them.** I ruled the Ray POP should hit a grid-topology surface "since it is analytic". WRONG: a grid is analytic ∈ its CONNECTIVITY, but its POSITIONS are arbitrary displaced buffers (E20's whole point) ∴ ⊥ closed-form intersection exists & brute force is P×2C — 4k points × 8k triangles @ 64² ≈ **33M tests per frame**. a uv-space grid walk would assume the surface is a GRAPH OVER UV, which displacement can break. 8th premise of mine corrected by someone reading the code.
V426: **REFUSE to auto-derive what the data cannot tell you.** shadow framing is an explicit `shadowExtent` rather than auto-fit from scene bounds, because SCENE PAYLOADS CARRY NO BOUNDS — an autofit would silently choose a wrong box & produce a plausible, wrongly-cropped shadow. an explicit number the user can see beats a derived one that is guessing.
V424: **∀ an AGENT, the SCHEMA IS THE DOCUMENTATION — ∴ a schema WIDER than its validator teaches a contract that does ⊥ exist.** a human reads prose & experiments; an agent reads the JSON schema & forms a call it believes is valid. our `ZodRecord` conversion drops the KEY type ∴ every record-keyed field advertises "any key" while enforcing an enum. the generic rule: **⊥ narrowing the generated schema silently WIDENS the published contract**, & the caller who trusts it has ⊥ way to discover the truth except by failing.
V421: **a SPEC ROW's STATUS is a CLAIM & it ROTS.** T480 was written by ME from T236's `~`, asserting `analyze`'s CPU half was unwired & E14 blocked. It was ⊥: T305 constructed it ∈ the composition root, `analyze-loop.test` drives it through the composed app, & `analyze.gpu.test` already walked image→number on Dawn. **the `~` was a spec row that lagged a landing** & I derived a task from it. ∴ I have been treating SPEC.md as GROUND TRUTH while writing tasks from it — a status is evidence about when someone last edited a line, ⊥ about the code. VERIFY A STATUS BEFORE BUILDING ON IT, exactly as V316 says to read an implementation rather than an invariant's wording.
V422: **RECOMPILING to change a value is ⊥ testing the VALUE PATH.** `backend.compile` of a values-only plan variation REUSES the program & does ⊥ rewrite uniforms — the ANIMATOR is the only path a per-frame value travels (V5 working as designed). ∴ a test that recompiles instead of pushing is SILENTLY ⊥ exercising the thing it names; the T480 author's own 1st draft was exactly that & the pixel stayed dark until it used the animator.
V418: **COUNT THE CONSUMERS of a currency before choosing where a name LIVES.** T479 put the channel NAME ∈ a `driven` parameter slot & only an ORDINAL ∈ the WGSL — because the reference currency has **FIVE** consumers: the rename rewrite, the strand count on label clear, `documentLiveness`'s producer walk, the editor's reference lines, & the dangling refusal. a name inside a shader blob would need ALL FIVE taught, & wiring FOUR is exactly B40. my lean was right ∀ a weaker reason (rename-safety); the count is the argument.
V419: a DERIVED CONVENIENCE that embeds a CHOICE ! ⊥ be derived. `ctx.dim` gives cols/rows/i/j & deliberately **⊥ u/v** — the DIVISOR is a choice: E20's sheet is an unwrapped grid whose kernel divides by `cols` on purpose, targeting a wrap claim made DOWNSTREAM, & a wrap-derived `u` would have silently folded column 63 onto column 0. give the NUMBERS; let the author write the division.
V416: a MAP over an existing slot MULTIPLIES; it does ⊥ REPLACE. T478's per-point colour is the geometry's TINT ∈ map mode — tint already means "multiplier on the material's base colour, white = inherit", so map mode is the SAME SENTENCE w/ 1 word changed. REPLACE would leave a material whose ALBEDO IS DEAD while its specular/roughness/maps stay live — HALF A MATERIAL, & V349's silent-ignore wearing a different face. multiply keeps every slot live & per-point colour visible AT ONCE, which is the lighting-AND-colour combination the survey found missing.
V417: **a REWRITE's diff is ⊥ DONE until the DOC beside it is GREPPED ∀ the old node names.** B83: the ports→references redirect rewrote E20's graph & its `.md` kept naming `renderSurface` 3 times. V332's OPERATIONAL form — ⊥ "docs drift", but the specific act that prevents it, performed as part of the rewrite rather than discovered later by a survey.
V413: **a TONE MAP w/ nothing to ROLL OFF is a GRADE, ⊥ a tone map.** rendered E4 under all 3 curves & LOOKED: `none` mean 165/max 252, `reinhard` 142/186, `filmic` 188/231. w/ ⊥ over-range values, Reinhard darkens the WHOLE FRAME & reads as "someone turned the brightness down" — ⊥ as highlight recovery. that is the case ∀ `none` as default, & **a byte test alone would ⊥ have surfaced it**: every number was correct & the picture was wrong ∀ the user's purpose. V383's 6th catch.
V414: **ENCODE & TONE MAP are ⊥ the same question & answering them APART is how B47 happened.** an `rgba8unorm-srgb` target ! ⊥ be encoded ∈ the shader & ! STILL be tone mapped — a transfer function does nothing about 4.0. ∴ 1 call returns BOTH halves (`sinkDisplayTransform`), & `sinkTargetSpace` is untouched because a CURVE compresses RANGE, ⊥ SPACE.
V415: a NON-VACUITY FLOOR that counts DOWN ! be DELETED when it reaches zero, ⊥ weakened to `>= 0`. `PLANNED_COMMANDS` is down to 8 & the menus' unbuilt-row count to 4; when the last planned row ships, the assertion & the disabled-item machinery go together — a floor of zero asserts nothing & reads like a gate.
V410: when an OWNER overrides a REASONED ruling, KEEP the reasoning & NAME the remedy — do ⊥ delete the argument w/ the code. T463's dim was mine ("a full-brightness render behind the patch makes the graph unreadable"); the owner overrode it ("touchdesigner is not doing that either") & TD's counterexample is REAL — their node chrome carries the contrast burden exactly as ours does. the ruling was **OUTVOTED, ⊥ DISPROVEN**, ∴ the dim was REMOVED rather than defaulted-to-1 & the CSS comment records where it goes if it returns. if readability complaints arrive behind a bright noise field, the fix is 1 line & the comment names it — & ⊥ one relitigates from scratch.
V408: **LOOPBACK IS ⊥ AUTHORISATION.** a WebSocket handshake is ⊥ same-origin-gated ∴ ANY site the user merely VISITS can open a socket to a localhost listener. binding 127.0.0.1 stops the LAN, ⊥ the browser. ∴ the PAIRING CODE is the actual gate — minted per process, published only through the host's own channels, typed by the human, never persisted, never ∈ a URL. ORIGIN is a 2nd fence (a browser cannot lie about it); ABSENT origin is accepted deliberately — a non-browser peer already runs AS the user & can read the code from the same log — & that weakening is documented @ the predicate.
V409: MINIMISE what runs ∈ a process an MCP CLIENT SPAWNS. the WebSocket server is ~200 hand-rolled lines on `node:http` rather than a dependency: a codec this repo can READ, ∈ a process the user's agent launches on their machine, beats a package we would ⊥ audit. widening that surface is a different risk from widening it ∈ the page.
V406: **a SURVEY that cannot FIND its subject says so.** "Sentinel" resolved only to a security product, a VPN, a film & an agent-testing tool across 5 framings — so the report describes ⊥ tool it could ⊥ locate. V355 honoured against the temptation to write something plausible about a name the owner supplied. a 30-second link from the owner closes it; a fabricated paragraph would ⊥ have.
V403: an ABSENCE we REPORT ! name what would MAKE it present. our panel said "this browser exposes no navigator.modelContext" — TRUE, USELESS, & wrong-namespaced: it told the owner a dead end w/o telling them Chrome 146+ has it behind a FLAG. ∴ a capability row states the capability, the reason it is absent, & the ROUTE to having it — else "Unavailable" reads as "impossible" (V338's sibling: show the detection result, & show what to DO about it).
V404: **PORT THE OLD TESTS UNCHANGED when replacing a subsystem** — they encode behaviour the new design FORGOT. the pane tree's `dockTab` returned a floated tab to the FIRST LEAF OF ITS ROLE; the ported V97 tests caught that the flat shell returned it to the leaf it LEFT. a reasonable-looking new rule, & only the old test knew otherwise. `PaneTab.home` restores it.
V405: **V386 vindicated END TO END — T405 built NOTHING.** 2 tabs of 1 role are 2 React instances BY CONSTRUCTION (portals keyed by minted key) ∴ the 2nd viewer's output picker was ALREADY independent local state; `present()` was ALREADY multi-surface, its own comment saying so. the "N viewers = N renders" cost cliff I briefed as the binding constraint did ⊥ exist. ∴ INVENTORY THE SEAMS BEFORE DESIGNING NEW GATING — 4th time this session that inventorying replaced building.
V401: ONE derived number doing THREE jobs is 3 bugs waiting. `stepFor` derives a step from a declared range when none is given, & that step is drag granularity AND display decimals AND the quantisation grid — so a plausible `{min:0,max:100}` on a CONTINUOUS parameter renders 0.25 as `0` & then COMMITS the 0. ∴ a parameter that says nothing about discreteness is CONTINUOUS & ! be able to show a fraction; an integer parameter SAYS SO. gated as a class, ⊥ fixed as an instance.
V402: **⊥ ERROR BOUNDARY existed ANYWHERE** — `componentDidCatch`/`getDerivedStateFromError` appeared ZERO times ∈ all of `src/` ∴ any render throw ∈ any surface unmounted the WHOLE React root: white screen, ⊥ message, & the unsaved graph gone w/ the tree that showed it. a crash that costs the user their work is worse than any single bug. per-pane containment now; & the honest limit is stated — a boundary catches RENDER/LIFECYCLE only, ⊥ an `onClick`/`onBlur` throw.
V399: a COMPATIBILITY PRESET is a migration COMFORT, ⊥ a design — & it expires. once the migration preserves the user's OWN arrangement (T466), a "here is how it used to look" preset offers a FRESH user nostalgia ∀ something they never used. delete it; but a preset that vanishes while SELECTED ! fall back gracefully & NAMED, ⊥ dangle. NOTE the tax it imposed may OUTLIVE it & be worth keeping — Classic forced panels-collapsible, which turned out generally right.
V400: BUILD ORDER follows GATE-ABILITY. the tree's split/close/role controls landed WITH the shell rather than after it, because **a tree ⊥ one can GROW is invisible** — the V383 geometry gate needs a way to SPLIT before it can measure a split. ∴ when a phase cannot be gated w/o the next phase's affordance, they are 1 phase.
V396: when defining a CAPABILITY predicate, enumerate how the capability is actually DELIVERED — ⊥ the 1 delivery you have ∈ mind. I said "keys on whether the node publishes a value PORT". `analyze` publishes a channel via **GPU READBACK**: ⊥ port, ⊥ hook. `publishesValueChannels()` ∴ takes 3 branches incl a declared `measuredChannel` flag. my 1-branch formulation would have SILENTLY DROPPED analyze's plot — the exact failure the re-key existed to prevent.
V397: buy EXACTNESS GEOMETRICALLY rather than asserting w/ TOLERANCE. T462's preview rig looks straight down -z & its fill light has ⊥ z component ∴ centre texels pin ARITHMETICALLY (unlit 255/0/0; light@1 → 204; a gnomon face to the f32-quantized byte). choosing the RIG so the maths is closed beats a tolerance that hides a real drift.
V398: pin a DELIBERATE NON-FEATURE on BOTH sides. `geometry` previews NOTHING on purpose — its shape is the upstream splat, its material is the material ball, & its unique contribution (the PAIRING) is what the render it feeds shows. asserted on the COMPILE side & the DISPLAY side so ⊥ one "fixes" it into existence. V377's rule (assert the intentional gap) applied to a capability rather than a keybinding.
V393: a DEFAULT ∀ a MISSING INPUT turns "I ⊥ KNOW" into a CONFIDENT WRONG CLAIM. T459's plot defaulted an absent frame time to 0 ∴ the playhead ASSERTED phase 0 on a curve that looked entirely alive — the shape TRUE, the position a LIE. now: draw the curve, draw ⊥ MARKER, & show "—" ⊥ a number the node never produced. distinct from V369 (a fallback MASKING a missing input): here the fallback FABRICATES a reading. ∴ ∀ derived display, ask what it shows when its input is ABSENT — & "⊥ nothing" is usually the answer.
V394: a PREDICATE's NAME is ⊥ its BEHAVIOUR — & I asserted one while CITING V316. I told the track to derive the pure/stateful split from `isValueSourceDefinition`; its body is `valueChannel !== undefined || valueEvaluate !== undefined` — the UNION, true ∀ a Lag as readily as an LFO. they READ IT & built `isPureValueSource` instead. 4th premise of mine corrected by measurement today, & the 1 I had the least excuse ∀.
V395: a DISTINCTION ⊥ shipped node EXERCISES is INVISIBLE through the catalogue, & a sensitivity break over it stays GREEN. swapping the split ∀ the union passed TWICE, because ⊥ node declares BOTH hooks ∴ the 2 predicates agree on everything that exists. ∴ CONSTRUCT the discriminating member ∈ the test rather than waiting ∀ it to ship — V321's lesson (content that instantiates ONCE hides the class) applied to a PREDICATE.
V391: **a SIZE MODEL nobody MEASURES is a guess w/ a docstring.** T460's derived node box was pinned against REAL `offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight` ∈ Chrome — 108 instances, 8 examples, EXACT match — & the measurement caught 2 wrong constants (hairlines added ON TOP of stated heights, when `box-sizing: border-box` puts them INSIDE). w/o it the gate goes GREEN while the examples overlap: the bug 1 level up. ∴ ∀ model of what the DOM renders, PIN IT to a measurement & keep the pin repeatable. (⊥ `boundingBox()` — the viewport SCALES; `offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight` is the GRAPH-SPACE box positions are authored ∈.)
V392: a GUTTER is PER-AXIS because the RISKS differ. vertical needs room ∀ a runtime `.message` row (~30px) that appears the moment the compiler complains — a graph LEGAL UNTIL SOMETHING ERRORS is ⊥ laid out. horizontal needs only what an edge & a port label occupy, because width is a CONSTANT & nothing widens a node @ runtime. 1 number ∀ both flagged perfectly readable columns & would have forced churn ∀ ⊥ reason.
V390: a BROKEN TEST HELPER takes a whole SUITE dark & the suite still REPORTS. B77's `openApp` waited on a selector nothing rendered ∴ every e2e spec failed ∀ the same reason, & "e2e is red" became BACKGROUND NOISE rather than a signal — through the exact window when a GEOMETRY change most needed it. ∴ a suite failing WHOLESALE ∀ 1 cause is an OUTAGE, ⊥ a set of failures; triage the SHARED cause 1st, & ⊥ landing is covered by a suite ⊥ one can currently read.
V385: DEGRADE LOUDLY ON READ, NEVER LOSSILY ON WRITE. a back-compat PROJECTION is written only while it is FAITHFUL; the moment the real shape outgrows it (2 viewers ∈ a 5-zone projection) the projection is **CLEARED, ⊥ left stale** — a stale projection is WORSE than an absent one, because an old build silently restores an arrangement the user abandoned & believes it succeeded. absent → the old build falls back to its default, which is VISIBLE.
V386: the COST CLIFF you fear may already be closed by seams you own. N viewers on 1 output was assumed to mean N renders; V64 already supports any number of SURFACES presenting one output (present per canvas, blit per frame, ⊥ readback) ∴ it is N BLITS. viewers on DIFFERENT outputs route through T252's preview-sink set, which ALREADY makes an unwatched target free & a watched one exactly 1 materialization shared app-wide. ∴ inventory the existing seams BEFORE designing new gating — & GATE the claim (2 viewers, 1 output → resource count unchanged).
V383: **a LOOK PASS is a REQUIRED gate ∀ any example, & it catches a class ⊥ test can.** rendered frames, inspected by a human, ⊥ a green suite. 4 instances, each STRUCTURALLY CORRECT & communicating NOTHING: E2's 1st feed/kill band (both ends a labyrinth ∴ the spatial map bought noise & ⊥ variety); E24's kick driving the lookup's ROW on a HORIZONTAL ramp (B71, a perfect no-op) & wind @ 0.22°/ITERATION killing the dish (B73); E20 as flat putty; E25's 1st render — **a floating torus & NO SCREEN**, because render A cleared to UNLIT BLACK & an unlit-black screen ∈ a dark room is INVISIBLE. every wire bound, every draw drawn, every assertion green. ∴ ⊥ example ships w/o someone LOOKING @ it, & the verdict goes ∈ the report.
V384: a RENDER ! paint a BACKGROUND, ⊥ clear to nothing. E25 proved it: an unlit-black surface composited into darkness is indistinguishable from an absent one, & the failure has ⊥ symptom anywhere ∈ the plan. a backdrop pass @ far depth w/ the colour as a VALUE; geometry composes over it & nothing else clears.
V379: AVOID a silent hazard BY CONSTRUCTION rather than GUARDING it. B33 (a storage-buffer limit breach is SILENT — pipeline creation fails, every dispatch no-ops, ⊥ diagnostic anywhere) was to be handled by counting usage against the budget. instead the lit shader carries NAMED vec4 MEMBERS per light: ⊥ storage buffer, ∴ ⊥ cliff to guard & ⊥ guard to get wrong. the best defence against a silent failure is ⊥ entering its domain.
V380: WGSL codegen notes, paid for: **`meta` is a RESERVED KEYWORD**; and **uniform ARRAY members defeat the uniform writer's REFLECTION** — NAMED members are the one shape every reflector agrees on. ∴ generated uniform blocks enumerate named members, & a count stays STRUCTURAL while contents stay VALUES.
V381: SCENE references COUNT ∈ the cycle gate; FEEDBACK references do ⊥. the distinction is the POINT of each: a feedback ref EXISTS to close a loop the DAG forbids, while scene assembly is ACYCLIC & a loop through it is a REAL ERROR. ∴ an exemption belongs to the KIND that needs it, ⊥ to references generally.
V375: a JUSTIFICATION written ∈ a COMMENT is a CLAIM. if ⊥ test covers it, it is an UNVERIFIED claim wearing the clothes of a verified 1 — & it reads as settled to every future maintainer. TWICE ∈ 1 session the author wrote a reason ∈ a comment & a sensitivity break showed nothing checked it (V123's frame count; `H` centring on CONTENT ⊥ origin, where the test passed under BOTH). ∴ writing "because X" ∈ a comment is the moment to ask what would FAIL if ⊥X.
V376: **COUNT OCCURRENCES BEFORE A REPLACE.** a scripted `replace` of `return holder;` hit TWO call sites & turned a lookup into a registrar — 2nd silent duplicate-block edit ∈ 1 day. a replace-all across a file is a mutation whose SCOPE is invisible ∈ the diff you INTENDED. check the count, | anchor on something unique.
V377: a DELIBERATE ABSENCE ∈ an otherwise-transcribed table is INDISTINGUISHABLE from an omission ∴ a future reader RESTORES it. `H`/`h`/`o` are gone from a TD-verified keymap, so `defaults.test.ts` now ASSERTS each is unbound WITH ITS REASON — 3 omissions turned into 3 DECISIONS. ∀ intentional gap ∈ a table that otherwise mirrors a source: assert the gap.
V378: **DEPRECATED ≠ NON-FUNCTIONAL, & a RELEASE DATE is ⊥ a MEASUREMENT.** I blocked T398 on primary-source research — author's deprecation notice, last release 2025-03-22, ⊥ credential ∈ the maintained fork — & concluded the paste-token flow "does ⊥ exist". the owner had ALREADY RUN IT successfully. the research was ACCURATE & the CONCLUSION was wrong: I inferred capability from METADATA where a 10-minute trial was available. ∴ before declaring a thing impossible, TRY IT — this is V206/B39's rule turned on my OWN verdicts, & it cost a day of the owner's actual goal.
V372: **GPU DATA flows on WIRES; SCENE ASSEMBLY flows by NAME.** pointsets & textures — incl material MAP textures, ∴ E25's virtual-screen stays a plain texture edge — are WIRED. which geometry, which camera, which lights, which material are NAMED. 1 line, & every future "port or reference?" question answers itself.
V373: REFERENCES are the AUTHORING surface; PORTS are the PLUMBING. T350 already resolves a name into a SYNTHESIZED REAL EDGE before validation ∴ scene refs are that, GENERALIZED: the node declares real (reference-fed, connect-refused) input ports, the compiler turns names into synthetic edges, & every edge mechanism runs UNCHANGED. 1 resolution mechanism, ⊥ 2 — which is why the ports→references redirect cost almost nothing.
V374: **a PATTERN cannot be kept COHERENT under RENAME.** `geo*` is ⊥ a reference, it is a QUERY: when `geo3` → `blob1` it silently LEAVES the match set, & when a stranger names their node `geoX` it silently JOINS. that is V320's silent-misbind class made into a FEATURE. TD lives w/ it; our entire name-integrity discipline exists ⊥ to. ∴ EXPLICIT token lists, LIST ORDER = draw/light order (user-controlled & deterministic, better than edge-sort), rewrite clause is token-wise replace. patterns ! answer this paragraph 1st.
V370: a GENERIC ENUMERATOR is ⊥ an invoker. the palette runs `run(entry.command)` — a VARIABLE — ∴ it NAMES nothing & cannot make a command-reachability gate vacuous. MEASURED: adding & removing a palette exclusion changed the failing set by EXACTLY ZERO. ∴ a command reachable ONLY from the palette is PRECISELY the bug the gate exists to find, & needs ⊥ special case.
V371: a PLANNED promise nothing SHOWS is a promise to ⊥ one. `ui.findInGraph` left `PLANNED_COMMANDS` when its binding was removed, because that list's rule is that a planned command ! be NAMED by something on screen. the promise lives ∈ the TASK; re-adding the binding is what restores it, & the gate then DEMANDS the entry return.
V368: ⊥ ship an INERT PORT. a declared input nothing honours is B60/V342 ∈ port form — it invites a wire, accepts it, & does nothing. ∴ a deferred feature has ⊥ port at all, & says so ∈ the node's DESCRIPTION; the port arrives WITH the code that reads it.
V369: a FALLBACK ! ⊥ MASK a missing input. render's ambient floor exists so an unlit scene is ⊥ black — but a scene w/ a non-unlit material & ⊥ light ! also DIAGNOSE (V288), else "dim but visible" is the plausible-wrong answer & the user hunts their material instead of their missing light edge.
V367: a test asserting an OVER-APPROXIMATION of an invariant will FALSELY REFUSE a legal new shape. feedback's swap-late test asserted "after every EFFECT" where V22's actual property is "after every PAIR READER" — & the substep region LEGALLY places unrelated downstream effects after the swap, so a correct new feature read as a violation. ∴ when a gate refuses a change you believe is legal, re-derive what the INVARIANT says before weakening the gate: the bug is ⊥ always ∈ the new code, & ⊥ always ∈ the test either — often the test asserts MORE than the rule.
V361: a connection can be STRUCTURALLY PERFECT & PROVABLY INERT. B71's kick was wired, compiled & bound, & drove a coordinate that could ⊥ matter — every structural assertion passes & the effect is exactly ZERO. distinct from V147 (the picture ⊥ move): here the picture moves, the graph is right, & 1 EDGE does nothing. ∴ ∀ a driven parameter, ask what OBSERVABLE would differ if this edge were CUT — & if the answer needs thought, the test ! assert that observable. ASSERTABLE FORM (T442): render the graph w/ the drive LIVE & again w/ it OFF, & demand the pixels DIFFER — the observable made a test rather than a question.
V362: a capability w/ ⊥ EXAMPLE is a capability NOTHING EXERCISES — & the bug it hides is ⊥ ∈ the feature, it is ∈ its INTEGRATION. `cache` had a per-frame bind-group allocation latent since T237, invisible to its own unit & Dawn tests, & the settled-allocation gate caught it the INSTANT an example contained one. examples are ⊥ documentation; they are the only INTEGRATION gate we have (B42, B39, B41, B72 all shipped ∀ this reason).
V357: define a RECORDED CONTRACT by the INTERVAL it describes, ⊥ by how it is currently COMPUTED — then improving the implementation is ⊥ a versioning event. T437's `onsetCount` = rising crossings WITHIN THE FRAME INTERVAL, `onsetMax` = the interval's PEAK. per-frame analysis makes them 0|1 today; a faster hop later reports real multi-event frames & the MEANING is unchanged ∴ ⊥ track version. had they been defined as "what the analyser returned", every fidelity improvement would have invalidated every recording. corollary of V352, & the way OUT of its cost.
V358: an OPTIONAL REGION ! exist even @ its identity value. substeps' loop region is emitted @ count 1, else 1→3 is a STRUCTURAL change & the animator refuses it — the region is STRUCTURE, the count is a VALUE, & collapsing the region when the count happens to be 1 destroys that distinction exactly when someone turns it up.
V354: "honest-absent" stops being honest when the SURFACE EXISTS. a planned command the palette marks unavailable is fine ∀ a feature ⊥ built; the same treatment ∀ a key naming a surface the user can SEE reads as a BROKEN app. ∴ the planned list ! be checked against what SHIPS, ⊥ only against what is registered.
V351: a fully-wired command can still be UNREACHABLE — REGISTRATION, BINDING & HANDLER ∀ correct, & the CONTEXT never matches. V342 said registration ⊥ prove invocability; this is the next layer: DISPATCH ⊥ prove REACHABILITY. a context-scoped binding needs the context to be ENTERABLE — ∴ whatever DECLARES a context ! also be able to HOLD FOCUS, & the gate ! press the key w/ focus where a USER would leave it (the canvas background), ⊥ where the test conveniently puts it.
V352: RECORD the DERIVED value that determined the output, ⊥ the SOURCE that produced it, when the derivation is ⊥ reproducible. replaying PCM would need re-analysis matching the browser `AnalyserNode`'s windowing & FFT BIT-FOR-BIT across engines — a V47 parity promise ⊥ one can keep. the FEATURES are what actually determined the render ∴ recording THEM makes replay exact BY CONSTRUCTION, incl frame COST (audio-driven substeps become a pure function of replayed numbers). COROLLARY: the derivation's SEMANTICS become the recorded CONTRACT — band edges & flux normalisation are pinned w/ exact values, & changing them is a VERSIONING event ∀ every recorded track, ⊥ a tweak.
V353: DETERMINISTIC SILENCE is ALL-ZEROS, ⊥ an ABSENT bag. a missing feature set would DANGLE every driven parameter; zeros keep the whole graph EVALUATING. an offline render w/o a track replays silence — the same silence every run, never re-listening (V329). **the all-zeros rule covers the ABSENT track; a LIVE mic (`audioIn`) during a take is the webcam problem w/ a different sensor — `external-live` per T645 — & is ⊥ made deterministic by this silence, only by RECORDING TO A FILE first.**
V347: a partially-working LOOP is the worst shape of V147 — it MOVES, so every "does it animate?" check passes, & only the RATE is wrong. B64's 3 substeps advanced 2. ∴ ∀ iterated work, assert an EQUATION only real iteration satisfies: **N frames @ K substeps ≡ N×K frames @ 1, to the byte**.
V348: an AESTHETIC property can only be judged by LOOKING, & ! be REDUCED to a measurable proxy before it can be gated. T388's 1st feed/kill band was too NARROW — both ends produced a labyrinth — & ⊥ test could have said so; it was caught by rendering PNGs & looking. the proxy that DID gate it: pin the animated input & count moving pixels (642 of 262144 = a STATIC MAZE). ∴ look 1st, then find the number that would have told you.
V346: a REGRESSION SIGNATURE ! be SEPARABLE — 1 per FACT the effect depends on, ⊥ 1 ∀ the effect. E20's surface survives ∀ 3 INDEPENDENT reasons & each has its own symptom: normal-aligned displacement (else FOLD ⊥ stretch), continuity ∈ space & time (white noise SHREDS it, a 2D noise type FREEZES it), & the seam being a CLAIM ⊥ geometry (delete topology & ⊥ point moves — the ball just shows a SLIT). "it looks wrong" is ⊥ a signature; 3 distinguishable symptoms are.
V345: a GLOBAL REGENERATOR is a CROSS-TRACK HAZARD. `build-examples` rewrites EVERY generated artifact from the CURRENT working tree ∴ a track regenerating ITS OWN example bakes every OTHER track's uncommitted state into shipped files — & the result LOOKS like a legitimate regeneration. ∀ regenerate-all tool ! take a SCOPE, & the default ! be the narrow one.
V344: a PROCESSOR RE-READS its input; it ⊥ INTEGRATE it. E16's flock binds `position` to the UPSTREAM sphere's pair as a fresh ANCHOR every frame, while `offset`/`velocity` live ∈ the kernel's OWN persistent pairs, & `position = anchor + offset`. ∴ 1 node is a PROCESSOR (⊥ drift, the anchor is authoritative) AND a SIMULATION (state accumulates ∈ the offset) — which is V197's mixed ownership doing real work rather than being a rule.
V343: a MISMATCH check is blind to a LONE input that is simply WRONG. comparing inputs to EACH OTHER catches disagreement & ⊥ a REQUIREMENT — a node needing linear & given encoded has nothing to disagree with. ∴ a node whose maths depend on a space ! DECLARE that requirement on its PORT, ⊥ rely on a cross-input comparison. & "absent ≡ linear" (V57) is an assumption the checker cannot verify.
V342: a command being REGISTERED proves ⊥ that it can be INVOKED. `node.rename` had a bus registration, a keybinding, a menu row & an ellipsis promising a prompt, & ⊥ surface anywhere collects the ARGUMENT it needs. ∴ ∀ command taking an argument the user ! supply, the gate ! find the SURFACE that supplies it, ⊥ merely the registration.
V340: IDENTITY ≠ ROLE. fusing them caps the system @ ONE of each & the cap is INVISIBLE until someone wants 2. `PaneId` names WHAT A PANE SHOWS & WHICH PANE IT IS ∈ one value ∴ a 2nd viewer is unrepresentable — ⊥ by a rule, but because there is ⊥ way to SAY it. ∀ singleton union used as a key: ask whether the user will ever want 2.
V339: a DOM-EXISTENCE assertion is ⊥ evidence a thing is VISIBLE. B54 lived ∈ the DOM w/ a real client rect & painted 0 pixels ∀ months, & the test asserting its presence was green the whole time — jsdom paints NOTHING, ∴ ∀ jsdom suite is blind to "renders | ⊥". anything whose defect is PAINTED-OR-NOT needs a GEOMETRIC precondition asserted (the layer's BOX, ⊥ the element) | a real browser. new V220 shape: wired, mounted, ∈ the DOM, & INVISIBLE.
V338: a FEATURE-DETECTED integration that registers NOWHERE is indistinguishable from a BROKEN one, from ∈ the app. `registerWebMcp` returns `{registered, toolCount}` & ⊥ one displays it ∴ the honest answer to "is the agent connected?" is currently unobtainable. ∀ optional integration: SHOW the detection result, ⊥ merely branch on it.
V335: a diagnostic emitted on a path the LIVE editor DISCARDS ⊥ reach a user. the per-frame re-resolve keeps only `.compiled` & drops diagnostics; the structural compile runs @ the ZERO frame where nothing clamps. ∴ a warning reached tests, gates & offline renders & ⊥ ONE person @ a keyboard. ask WHERE a diagnostic is CONSUMED, ⊥ whether it is emitted.
V336: WRAP needs a PERIOD; a manifest declares a RANGE. ±360 is TWO periods wide ∴ "wrap into the declared range" has ⊥ single answer — 370 → 10 | −350, both defensible. ∴ ⊥ silently reinterpret a range as a period; make the author say `mod(x, 360)` & state the intent.
V337: V186b applies to TESTS, ⊥ only docs. `concepts.test.ts` asserted a clamp warns; the code could ⊥ emit that warning; the test passed anyway. a test can encode a claim that was NEVER true — & then it LOOKS like the claim is gated.
V334: a cleanup that closes a SHARED, NAMED resource ! verify it still OWNS it. StrictMode's mount→cleanup→mount, + `window.open` REUSING a window by name, means cleanup A's deferred close lands on mount B's window. ∴ guard by OWNERSHIP TOKEN (⊥ by `closed`, ⊥ by a timer): close ONLY if ⊥ newer mount has claimed the name.
V331: a DERIVED number ∈ prose is gated by ⊥ANYTHING ∴ it ROTS SILENTLY. E8's 2 wrong figures survived because ⊥ test computes them. ∴ either COMPUTE the figure ∈ a test | ⊥ state it. (V206: I put ~0.046 ∈ the spec; measured 0.0376.)
V332: when a CONVENTION changes, the doc written NEXT gets it right & EVERY doc written BEFORE keeps the old claim — & looks authoritative. ∴ a convention change ! SWEEP prior docs, ⊥ merely document itself going forward. E12 explains feedback-by-reference; E1 & E2 still described the wired back-edge that ⊥ exists.
V333: a test asking the WRONG HARNESS reports ABSENCE as truth. the menus' test asked a bare DOMAIN bus whether `component.publishParameter` was live; component commands are registered on the APP bus ∴ a real command since T132 read as PLANNED ∀ months. "⊥ found" ≡ "⊥ there" ONLY if the place you looked is the place it lives.
V328: state the CAPABILITY, ⊥ promise the HARDWARE. a browser cannot address the Apple Neural Engine directly; WebNN → CoreML → ANE is the only route & exists only where implemented. detect & REPORT what is actually available. (V256: I once wrote "desktop adapters offer 10-64+" from nothing — Dawn/Metal offers 10.)
V329: an ASYNC result ∈ a per-frame graph ! expose its STALENESS. a node silently showing a result from 400ms ago is the V147 family again: the picture is plausible & wrong. & OFFLINE/SEEK render ! ⊥ depend on wall-clock arrival — same project, 2 renders, 2 outputs is ⊥ acceptable ∴ block, | declare non-reproducible loudly. **ENUMERATED & derived-against ∈ `domain/render/reproducibility.ts` (T645): `async-cached` = `analyze`; `external-live` = `webcam`, `audioIn`, `mouse` — the last 2 found by THE CLASSIFICATION ITSELF, ⊥ by any report.**
V330: a VIEWER camera is VIEW STATE (V15) ∴ ⊥ reach the document. & its RESET ! derive from CONTENT BOUNDS, ⊥ a constant — a "reset view" tuned on 1 scene strands the user off-screen ∈ every other.
V325: an EXPLICIT name ! be REFUSED on collision, ⊥ SUFFIXED. a caller carrying a label has references written against EXACTLY that label ∴ silently minting `over1_2` re-creates the dangle V324 exists to kill. suffixing is ∀ AUTO-names only. likewise an explicit field holding EMPTY ⊥ mean "auto" — that is a caller mistake & refuses by name.
V326: a CLIPBOARD OUTLIVES its paste ∴ a rewrite over copied nodes ! own FRESH parameter records. same footgun as the detached-component share (B41): rewriting shared records edits the SOURCE of the NEXT paste. ∀ copy-then-rewrite: clone the records 1st.
V327: refs LEAVING a copied selection stay on the ORIGINAL, BY DESIGN — half a chain pasted beside its driver ! still be driven. only INTRA-selection refs follow the copy. the boundary is a DECISION & ! be pinned by its own test, else a later "fix" will rewrite ∀ refs & silently orphan the paste from its inputs.
V323: same CLASS ≠ same MECHANISM. enumerate a class's members by their FAILURE SHAPE, ⊥ by the fix that worked on member #1. V320's survey: flatten & detach COPY labels verbatim (collision), paste DROPS them (rebind-to-original) — inverted mechanisms, 1 class, & the member-1 fix transfers to #2 & ⊥ to #3. ∴ a class fix is ⊥ done until each member is named w/ its own shape.
V324: an op whose IDENTITY is minted @ APPLY time from MUTABLE graph state is ⊥ REPLAYABLE. `addNode`'s auto-name RECLAIMS names deleted nodes held ∴ undo-then-redo can mint DIFFERENT labels & any reference written against the 1st set dangles. ops that create named things ! carry the name.
V322: a MOCK host that executes ⊥ shader ∴ shader TEXT is unvalidated ∀ suite built on it. structural tests ⊥ substitute ∀ COMPILATION. ∀ shipped WGSL ! be built on a real device by some gate, & that gate's SENSITIVITY ! be proven by breaking 1 shader & watching exactly 1 test go red.
V320: if NAMES are the reference currency (V129), then ∀ operation that DUPLICATES a subgraph ! rewrite them. duplicate-then-resolve-by-name = FIRST-WINS silent misbind, ⊥ error — the 2nd copy's refs quietly drive the 1st copy's nodes. applies to flatten/instancing/paste/import alike.
V321: content that instantiates ONCE hides a whole class of duplication bug. "∀ shipped examples pass" is ⊥ evidence ∀ any property that only breaks @ COPY #2. ∴ ∀ duplicable unit, the gate ! instantiate it TWICE.
V318: "the REGISTRAR MODULE is reachable" ⊥ evidence — a barrel `index.ts` makes ∀ sibling module reachable. rule ! be the factory half's: an exported FUNCTION of the registrar ! be REFERENCED by another reachable module. consts ⊥ count (importing `OPEN_SETTINGS_COMMAND` ∀ a label says nothing about registration). PROVE by unwiring & watching guard go red.
V319: enumerate surfaces by the PRIMITIVE they render (`DialogRoot`), ⊥ by NAME SUFFIX. measured: 5 modules where `Pane|Panel|Dialog` suffixes found 4 — the miss was `CommandPalette`, which ⊥ naming convention would ever catch. a rule over CONSTRUCTION catches the surface nobody named conventionally; a rule over names catches only the disciplined.
V316: an invariant stated over a CATEGORY but implemented over one MEMBER silently NARROWS when member #2 appears. V128 said "∀ reference naming that node"; code scanned `bindings.expression` alone, so `driven` channels (V129, same currency) were never covered & ⊥ one noticed for months. ∴ when adding reference kind #N, ⊥ ask "does invariant cover this?" — read what its IMPLEMENTATION enumerates. structure as CLAUSE-PER-KIND, ⊥ special cases, so kind #N+1 ! declare itself.
V317: a deliberate SEVERITY DOWNGRADE makes every adjacent hole SILENT. unattached channel = info ("reserved, ⊥ broken") is CORRECT ∀ Phase-2 & is exactly why B40 shows ⊥ diagnostic. ∴ when downgrading a diagnostic, enumerate what ELSE it was incidentally covering; 2 individually-right decisions compose into a silent failure.
V314: an INHERITED exclusion carries its REASON; re-derive that reason against CURRENT design ⊥ inherit it. T300 excluded renderer predicates because "vertex stage sees only what that renderer binds" — but narrowness was a fact about the EDGE (untyped pairs → auto-bind = guess), ⊥ about vertex stage. V308's typed payload deleted it & ⊥ one noticed. ∴ before honouring past scope-out, ask WHICH FACT made it true & whether that fact still holds.
V315: a PREDICATE referencing ⊥ attribute → REFUSE. gating ∀ points on constant = bypass wearing costume; `1.0 > 0.5` silently drawing everything (| nothing) ∈ plausible-wrong class (V288).
V313: MAPPED COLOURS are LINEAR BY DECLARATION — a point attribute is DATA (V56/V57), ⊥ display-space colour. a `color`-QUALIFIED attribute (V284) is precisely what a future colour-space op would convert & what a SPATIAL transform ! leave alone; that pairing is the qualifier's whole purpose & belongs ∈ its write-up.
V310: **HYSTERESIS, ⊥ SMOOTHING.** a slow decay is still CONTINUOUS MOTION, which is the thing V296 forbids — it makes the breathing slower, ⊥ absent. the range changes ⊥ AT ALL while the window fits (same object, so a caller can compare by identity), refits AT ONCE when it escapes, & quantises OUTWARD to a 1-2-5 step so warm-up — where the window grows a sample @ a time & technically escapes every frame — lands on the same bounds instead of a hundred of them.
V311: **a flag whose MEANING changes needs migration ∈ BOTH directions, & the FALSE case can be the dangerous one.** `preview: true` meant PINNED → becomes `previewPinned`. `preview: false` meant NOT pinned — i.e. the default — & read as a SWITCH it means OFF ∴ every node anyone had ever pinned & unpinned would open w/ its preview DISABLED & a dark slot. dropped, ⊥ carried. the `true` case is the one you think about; the `false` case is the bug report you get next week.
V312: **count the BUG'S SIGNATURE, ⊥ the fix's presence.** per-frame auto-ranging pins BOTH extremes to the box every frame BY CONSTRUCTION ∴ "0 of 40 sampled frames had both extremes pinned" measures the bug's absence directly, ∈ the running app, rather than asserting the new code ran.
V308: the edge PAYLOAD carries the attribute's TYPE — producers declare it, consumers SWIZZLE FROM IT, never from a guess. 3rd payload fact after half & count, & the 1 T287's qualifiers will want there too once transforms arrive. a consumer inferring type from a NAME is the "index as identity" mistake wearing a different hat.
V309: **"costs nothing by existing" is a SUSTAINED property, ⊥ a 1-off.** 4 optional stages now sit on the point kernels & renderer — group, spawn count, hook, map — & w/ none declared the generated WGSL is BYTE-IDENTICAL to what shipped before any of them. established @ T300 & re-checked @ every addition since. it is what makes the family safe to keep growing, & it stops being true the 1st time someone ⊥ check.
 EARNED ITS KEEP THE HOUR IT WAS WRITTEN, against its own author: T364's first draft routed colour through the VERTEX STAGE even unmapped, silently changing the shipped shader's text — every existing `renderPoints` would have structurally recompiled ONCE & ⊥ one would have known why. caught ONLY because the property was a STATED invariant to check rather than a habit. "it stops being true the 1st time somebody ⊥ check" was ⊥ hypothetical.
V307: **an OPENABLE SURFACE is opened by a COMMAND.** every one ∈ this app already is — help, node info, the libraries, the preview lens — EXCEPT the settings dialog, which opens from a `useState` toggle & is therefore unreachable from the palette | a keybinding. making that the RULE does 2 things: it gives every surface 3 doors (button, palette, key) for the price of one (V78), & it turns T356's weak half into a REGISTRY. the guard currently enumerates dialogs & settings by NAMING CONVENTION — measured honestly: 88 exported components, 63 matching ⊥ family, so a broader check gets switched off within a week — & a surface called `ShortcutEditor` slips straight through. against the COMMAND registry there is nothing to slip through.
